===== .\src\app\(app)\approvals\rules\escalation-settings.tsx =====
"use client";

import { useState, useTransition } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { Clock, Play, Zap } from "lucide-react";
import { Switch } from "@/components/approvals/switch";
import { runEscalationAction, setEscalationAction } from "../extra-actions";

type Rule = { id: string; code: string; name: string; days: number | null };

export function EscalationSettings({ rules }: { rules: Rule[] }) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [defaultDays, setDefaultDays] = useState("3");
  const [edits, setEdits] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [msg, setMsg] = useState<{ ok: boolean; text: string } | null>(null);
  const [pending, start] = useTransition();
  const anyOn = rules.some((r) => r.days !== null);

  async function apply(list: { id: string; days: string }[]) {
    for (const x of list) {
      const res = await setEscalationAction(x.id, x.days);
      if (!res.ok) return res.error;
    }
    return null;
  }

  function validDefault() {
    const n = Number(defaultDays);
    if (!Number.isInteger(n) || n < 1 || n > 60) {
      setMsg({ ok: false, text: "Days must be a whole number between 1 and 60" });
      return false;
    }
    return true;
  }

  function master(on: boolean) {
    setMsg(null);
    if (on && !validDefault()) return;
    start(async () => {
      const targets = on
        ? rules.filter((r) => r.days === null).map((r) => ({ id: r.id, days: defaultDays }))
        : rules.filter((r) => r.days !== null).map((r) => ({ id: r.id, days: "" }));
      const err = await apply(targets);
      if (err) setMsg({ ok: false, text: err });
      else {
        setEdits({});
        router.refresh();
      }
    });
  }

  function one(r: Rule, days: string) {
    setMsg(null);
    if (days !== "") {
      const n = Number(days);
      if (!Number.isInteger(n) || n < 1 || n > 60) {
        setMsg({ ok: false, text: "Days must be a whole number between 1 and 60" });
        return;
      }
    }
    start(async () => {
      const err = await apply([{ id: r.id, days }]);
      if (err) setMsg({ ok: false, text: err });
      else {
        setEdits((p) => {
          const n = { ...p };
          delete n[r.id];
          return n;
        });
        router.refresh();
      }
    });
  }

  function runNow() {
    setMsg(null);
    start(async () => {
      const res = await runEscalationAction();
      if (!res.ok) setMsg({ ok: false, text: res.error });
      else setMsg({ ok: true, text: `Escalation check done: ${res.data.escalated} request(s) escalated` });
      if (res.ok) router.refresh();
    });
  }

  return (
    <section id="escalation" className="rounded-card border bg-card p-6 shadow-card">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <Zap className="h-8 w-8 text-blue-600" fill="currentColor" fillOpacity={0.2} />
          <div>
            <h2 className="font-heading text-xl font-semibold text-navy-800">Escalation</h2>
            <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">Set up escalation rules for pending approvals</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <label className="flex items-center gap-3 text-sm font-semibold text-navy-800">
          Enable Escalation
          <Switch checked={anyOn} onChange={master} disabled={pending || rules.length === 0} label="Enable escalation for all rules" />
        </label>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-blue-50/70 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-navy-800">
        <Clock className="h-5 w-5 text-blue-600" />
        If a request waits more than
        <input
          value={defaultDays}
          onChange={(e) => setDefaultDays(e.target.value)}
          inputMode="numeric"
          aria-label="Default days"
          className="h-8 w-14 rounded-md border bg-white px-2 text-center text-sm outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-200"
        />
        days at a step, the next level is notified. Turn the switch off = off.
      </div>

      {rules.length === 0 ? (
        <p className="mt-4 text-sm text-muted-foreground">No rules yet. Save a rule above first.</p>
      ) : (
        <ul className="mt-4 divide-y">
          {rules.map((r) => {
            const shown = edits[r.id] ?? String(r.days ?? "");
            const dirty = edits[r.id] !== undefined && edits[r.id] !== String(r.days ?? "");
            return (
              <li key={r.id} className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 py-3 text-sm">
                <span className="min-w-48 flex-1">
                  <span className="font-semibold text-navy-800">{r.name}</span>
                  <span className="ml-2 text-xs text-slate-500">{r.code}</span>
                </span>
                <Switch
                  checked={r.days !== null}
                  disabled={pending}
                  label={`Escalation for ${r.name}`}
                  onChange={(on) => (on ? (validDefault() ? one(r, defaultDays) : undefined) : one(r, ""))}
                />
                <input
                  value={shown}
                  disabled={r.days === null}
                  onChange={(e) => setEdits((p) => ({ ...p, [r.id]: e.target.value }))}
                  inputMode="numeric"
                  aria-label={`Escalation days for ${r.name}`}
                  className="h-9 w-16 rounded-md border bg-white px-2 text-center outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-200 disabled:bg-slate-100"
                />
                <span className="text-slate-500">days</span>
                {dirty && (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    disabled={pending}
                    onClick={() => one(r, shown)}
                    className="rounded-md bg-primary px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-primary-foreground disabled:opacity-60"
                  >
                    Save
                  </button>
                )}
              </li>
            );
          })}
        </ul>
      )}

      <div className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
        <button
          type="button"
          disabled={pending}
          onClick={runNow}
          className="inline-flex h-10 items-center gap-2 rounded-full border bg-card px-5 text-sm font-semibold text-navy-800 hover:bg-slate-50 disabled:opacity-60"
        >
          <Play className="h-4 w-4" fill="currentColor" /> Run escalation check now
        </button>
        {msg && <p className={`text-sm ${msg.ok ? "text-emerald-700" : "text-destructive"}`}>{msg.text}</p>}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

===== .\src\app\(app)\approvals\rules\page.tsx =====
import Link from "next/link";
import { ArrowLeft } from "lucide-react";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { can, requireUser } from "@/lib/permissions";
import { approvalService } from "@/services/approval.service";
import { RuleForm } from "./rule-form";
import { EscalationSettings } from "./escalation-settings";

export default async function ApprovalRulesPage() {
  const user = await requireUser();
  if (!can(user, "approval.manage")) {
    return <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">You do not have permission to manage approval rules.</p>;
  }

  const [roles, workflows] = await Promise.all([
    prisma.role.findMany({ select: { id: true, name: true }, orderBy: { name: "asc" } }),
    approvalService.listWorkflows(),
  ]);

  return (
    <div className="stagger space-y-5">
      <Link
        href="/approvals"
        className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sm font-medium text-blue-600 hover:underline"
      >
        <ArrowLeft className="h-4 w-4" /> Back to approvals
      </Link>
      <RuleForm roles={roles} />
      <EscalationSettings
        rules={workflows.map((w) => ({ id: w.id, code: w.code, name: w.name, days: w.escalateAfterDays }))}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

===== .\src\app\(app)\approvals\rules\rule-form.tsx =====
"use client";

import { useState, useTransition } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { Loader2, Plus, RotateCcw, Send, ShieldCheck, Trash2 } from "lucide-react";
import { humanize } from "@/components/approvals/ui";
import { createWorkflowAction } from "../actions";

const ENTITY_TYPES = [
  "PurchaseOrder",
  "PurchaseRequisition",
  "CostSheet",
  "Shipment",
  "SampleRequest",
  "MaterialIssue",
  "StockAdjustment",
  "CreditNote",
];

const field =
  "h-11 w-full rounded-lg border bg-card px-3 text-sm outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-200";

function Lbl({ text, required, hint, children }: { text: string; required?: boolean; hint?: string; children: React.ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <label className="block">
      <span className="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-semibold text-navy-800">
        {text} {required && <span className="text-red-500">*</span>}
      </span>
      {children}
      {hint && <span className="mt-1 block text-xs text-slate-500">{hint}</span>}
    </label>
  );
}

export function RuleForm({ roles }: { roles: { id: string; name: string }[] }) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [steps, setSteps] = useState<string[]>(["", ""]); // prottek step-er approver role id
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [pending, start] = useTransition();

  function onSubmit(e: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    e.preventDefault();
    const fd = new FormData(e.currentTarget);
    const get = (k: string) => String(fd.get(k) ?? "");
    setError("");
    if (steps.some((s) => !s)) {
      setError("Select an approver role for every step");
      return;
    }
    start(async () => {
      const res = await createWorkflowAction({
        code: get("code"),
        name: get("name"),
        entityType: get("entityType"),
        minAmount: get("minAmount"),
        maxAmount: get("maxAmount"),
        steps: steps.map((approverRoleId, i) => ({ name: `Step ${i + 1}`, approverRoleId })),
      });
      if (!res.ok) {
        setError(res.error);
        return;
      }
      router.push("/approvals");
    });
  }

  return (
    <form
      onSubmit={onSubmit}
      onReset={() => {
        setSteps(["", ""]);
        setError("");
      }}
      className="overflow-hidden rounded-card border bg-card shadow-card"
    >
      {/* header */}
      <div className="flex items-center gap-4 bg-linear-to-r from-blue-50 to-white p-5">
        <span className="flex h-16 w-16 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl border-2 border-blue-500 bg-white text-blue-600">
          <ShieldCheck className="h-8 w-8" fill="currentColor" fillOpacity={0.2} />
        </span>
        <div>
          <h1 className="font-heading text-2xl font-semibold text-navy-800">Add Approval Rule</h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">Create a new approval rule for your workflow</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-5 p-6">
        <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-[1fr_1fr_1.1fr_1fr_1fr]">
          <Lbl text="Code" required>
            <input name="code" maxLength={30} placeholder="e.g. AR-001" className={field} />
          </Lbl>
          <Lbl text="Name" required>
            <input name="name" maxLength={100} placeholder="Enter rule name" className={field} />
          </Lbl>
          <Lbl text="Applies to" required>
            <select name="entityType" defaultValue="PurchaseOrder" className={field}>
              {ENTITY_TYPES.map((t) => (
                <option key={t} value={t}>
                  {humanize(t)}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </Lbl>
          <Lbl text="From amount" hint="Blank = 0">
            <input name="minAmount" inputMode="decimal" placeholder="e.g. 0" className={field} />
          </Lbl>
          <Lbl text="Up to (not incl.)" hint="Blank = no limit">
            <input name="maxAmount" inputMode="decimal" placeholder="e.g. 10000" className={field} />
          </Lbl>
        </div>

        {/* steps */}
        <div className="rounded-xl bg-slate-50 p-5">
          <p className="mb-4 text-sm font-semibold text-navy-800">Steps, in order</p>
          <div>
            {steps.map((roleId, i) => (
              <div key={i} className="flex gap-4">
                <div className="flex w-8 shrink-0 flex-col items-center">
                  <span className="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-blue-600 text-sm font-semibold text-white shadow">
                    {i + 1}
                  </span>
                  {i < steps.length - 1 && <span className="my-1 w-0.5 flex-1 bg-blue-300" />}
                </div>
                <div className="flex flex-1 flex-wrap gap-3 pb-3">
                  <select
                    value={roleId}
                    onChange={(e) => setSteps((s) => s.map((x, j) => (j === i ? e.target.value : x)))}
                    aria-label={`Approver role for step ${i + 1}`}
                    className={`${field} min-w-56 flex-1 bg-white`}
                  >
                    <option value="">Select approver role...</option>
                    {roles.map((r) => (
                      <option key={r.id} value={r.id}>
                        {r.name}
                      </option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    disabled={steps.length === 1}
                    onClick={() => setSteps((s) => s.filter((_, j) => j !== i))}
                    className="inline-flex h-11 items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border bg-white px-4 text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 transition-colors hover:bg-red-50 disabled:opacity-50"
                  >
                    <Trash2 className="h-4 w-4 text-red-500" /> Remove
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setSteps((s) => [...s, ""])}
            className="mt-1 flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-lg border-2 border-dashed border-blue-200 bg-white/60 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-blue-600 transition-colors hover:bg-blue-50"
          >
            <Plus className="h-4 w-4" /> Add step
          </button>
        </div>

        {error && <p className="text-sm text-destructive">{error}</p>}

        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
          <button
            type="reset"
            className="inline-flex h-11 items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border bg-card px-5 text-sm font-semibold text-navy-800 hover:bg-slate-50"
          >
            <RotateCcw className="h-4 w-4" /> Reset
          </button>
          <button
            type="submit"
            disabled={pending}
            className="inline-flex h-11 items-center gap-2 rounded-lg bg-primary px-6 text-sm font-semibold text-primary-foreground shadow-md shadow-blue-900/20 disabled:opacity-60"
          >
            {pending ? <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> : <Send className="h-4 w-4" />}
            Save Rule
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}

===== .\src\app\(app)\approvals\actions.ts =====
"use server";

import { revalidatePath } from "next/cache";
import { runAction } from "@/lib/action-result";
import { approvalService } from "@/services/approval.service";

function refresh() {
  revalidatePath("/approvals");
  revalidatePath("/", "layout"); // à¦Ÿà¦ªà¦¬à¦¾à¦°à§‡à¦° à¦˜à¦£à§à¦Ÿà¦¾
}

export async function decideApprovalAction(id: string, decision: "APPROVED" | "REJECTED", note: string) {
  return runAction(async () => {
    await approvalService.decide(id, decision, note.trim() || undefined);
    refresh();
    return { id };
  });
}

export async function cancelApprovalAction(id: string) {
  return runAction(async () => {
    await approvalService.cancel(id);
    refresh();
    return { id };
  });
}

export async function createWorkflowAction(values: {
  code: string;
  name: string;
  entityType: string;
  minAmount: string;
  maxAmount: string;
  steps: { name: string; approverRoleId: string }[];
}) {
  return runAction(async () => {
    const row = await approvalService.createWorkflow({
      code: values.code.trim(),
      name: values.name.trim(),
      entityType: values.entityType,
      minAmount: values.minAmount.trim() ? Number(values.minAmount) : undefined,
      maxAmount: values.maxAmount.trim() ? Number(values.maxAmount) : undefined,
      steps: values.steps.map((s) => ({ name: s.name.trim(), approverRoleId: s.approverRoleId })),
    });
    refresh();
    return { id: row.id };
  });
}

===== .\src\app\(app)\approvals\approval-buttons.tsx =====
"use client";

import { useState, useTransition } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { cancelApprovalAction, decideApprovalAction } from "./actions";

export function ApprovalButtons({ id, mode }: { id: string; mode: "decide" | "cancel" }) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [pending, start] = useTransition();

  function run(fn: () => Promise<{ ok: boolean; error?: string }>) {
    setError("");
    start(async () => {
      const res = await fn();
      if (!res.ok) setError(res.error ?? "Failed");
      else router.refresh();
    });
  }

  function approve() {
    run(() => decideApprovalAction(id, "APPROVED", ""));
  }

  function reject() {
    const reason = window.prompt("Why is this being rejected?");
    if (reason === null) return;
    if (reason.trim().length < 3) {
      setError("A reason is required (at least 3 characters)");
      return;
    }
    run(() => decideApprovalAction(id, "REJECTED", reason));
  }

  function cancel() {
    if (!window.confirm("Cancel this request?")) return;
    run(() => cancelApprovalAction(id));
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-1">
      {mode === "decide" ? (
        <div className="flex gap-1">
          <Button size="sm" disabled={pending} onClick={approve}>
            Approve
          </Button>
          <Button size="sm" variant="destructive" disabled={pending} onClick={reject}>
            Reject
          </Button>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <Button size="sm" variant="outline" disabled={pending} onClick={cancel}>
          Cancel
        </Button>
      )}
      {error && <p className="text-xs text-destructive">{error}</p>}
    </div>
  );
}

===== .\src\app\(app)\approvals\delegation-panel.tsx =====
"use client";

import { useState, useTransition } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { Loader2, Send, Users } from "lucide-react";
import { Panel } from "@/components/dashboard/widgets";
import { delegateAction, revokeDelegationAction } from "./extra-actions";

const field =
  "h-11 w-full rounded-lg border bg-card px-3 text-sm outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-200";

type Del = { id: string; who: string; start: string; end: string; reason: string; active: boolean };

function Lbl({ text, children }: { text: string; children: React.ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <label className="block">
      <span className="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-semibold text-navy-800">{text}</span>
      {children}
    </label>
  );
}

function Row({ d, onRevoke, busy }: { d: Del; onRevoke?: () => void; busy?: boolean }) {
  return (
    <li className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 rounded-lg bg-slate-50 px-3 py-2 text-sm">
      <span>
        <span className="font-semibold text-navy-800">{d.who}</span>
        <span className="ml-2 text-xs text-slate-500">
          {d.start} to {d.end} Â· {d.reason}
        </span>
      </span>
      <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
        <span
          className={`rounded-full px-2.5 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-semibold ${
            d.active ? "bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700" : "bg-slate-200 text-slate-600"
          }`}
        >
          {d.active ? "Active" : "Revoked"}
        </span>
        {onRevoke && d.active && (
          <button
            type="button"
            disabled={busy}
            onClick={onRevoke}
            className="rounded-md border bg-card px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-medium hover:bg-slate-100 disabled:opacity-60"
          >
            Revoke
          </button>
        )}
      </span>
    </li>
  );
}

export function DelegationPanel(props: {
  users: { id: string; label: string }[];
  given: Del[];
  received: Del[];
  defaultStart: string;
  defaultEnd: string;
}) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [f, setF] = useState({ toUserId: "", startDate: props.defaultStart, endDate: props.defaultEnd, reason: "" });
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [pending, start] = useTransition();

  function run(fn: () => Promise<{ ok: boolean; error?: string }>, after?: () => void) {
    setError("");
    start(async () => {
      const res = await fn();
      if (!res.ok) setError(res.error ?? "Failed");
      else {
        after?.();
        router.refresh();
      }
    });
  }

  return (
    <Panel
      icon={Users}
      iconClass="bg-blue-100 text-blue-600"
      title="Delegation"
      subtitle="Delegate your approval rights to another person"
      className="h-full"
    >
      <div className="grid items-end gap-3 md:grid-cols-[1.4fr_1fr_1fr_1.4fr_auto]">
        <Lbl text="Delegate to">
          <select className={field} value={f.toUserId} onChange={(e) => setF({ ...f, toUserId: e.target.value })}>
            <option value="">Select user...</option>
            {props.users.map((u) => (
              <option key={u.id} value={u.id}>
                {u.label}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </Lbl>
        <Lbl text="From">
          <input type="date" className={field} value={f.startDate} onChange={(e) => setF({ ...f, startDate: e.target.value })} />
        </Lbl>
        <Lbl text="To">
          <input type="date" className={field} value={f.endDate} onChange={(e) => setF({ ...f, endDate: e.target.value })} />
        </Lbl>
        <Lbl text="Reason (Optional)">
          <input className={field} placeholder="Enter reason..." value={f.reason} onChange={(e) => setF({ ...f, reason: e.target.value })} />
        </Lbl>
        <button
          type="button"
          disabled={pending}
          onClick={() =>
            run(
              () => delegateAction({ ...f, reason: f.reason.trim() || "Delegated" }),
              () => setF((p) => ({ ...p, toUserId: "", reason: "" }))
            )
          }
          className="inline-flex h-11 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-lg bg-primary px-5 text-sm font-semibold text-primary-foreground disabled:opacity-60"
        >
          {pending ? <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> : <Send className="h-4 w-4" />}
          Delegate
        </button>
      </div>
      {error && <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-destructive">{error}</p>}

      <div className="mt-5 grid gap-4 border-t pt-4 md:grid-cols-2">
        <div>
          <p className="mb-2 text-sm font-semibold text-navy-800">I delegated</p>
          {props.given.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">None</p>
          ) : (
            <ul className="space-y-1.5">
              {props.given.map((d) => (
                <Row key={d.id} d={d} busy={pending} onRevoke={() => run(() => revokeDelegationAction(d.id))} />
              ))}
            </ul>
          )}
        </div>
        <div>
          <p className="mb-2 text-sm font-semibold text-navy-800">Delegated to me</p>
          {props.received.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">None</p>
          ) : (
            <ul className="space-y-1.5">
              {props.received.map((d) => (
                <Row key={d.id} d={d} />
              ))}
            </ul>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </Panel>
  );
}

===== .\src\app\(app)\approvals\extra-actions.ts =====
"use server";

import { runAction } from "@/lib/action-result";
import { approvalService } from "@/services/approval.service";

export async function delegateAction(values: { toUserId: string; startDate: string; endDate: string; reason: string }) {
  return runAction(async () => {
    const row = await approvalService.delegate(values);
    return { id: row.id };
  });
}

export async function revokeDelegationAction(id: string) {
  return runAction(async () => {
    await approvalService.revokeDelegation(id);
    return { id };
  });
}

export async function setEscalationAction(workflowId: string, days: string) {
  return runAction(async () => {
    await approvalService.setEscalation(workflowId, days.trim() === "" ? null : Number(days));
    return { id: workflowId };
  });
}

export async function runEscalationAction() {
  return runAction(async () => {
    const r = await approvalService.escalateOverdue();
    return { escalated: r.escalated };
  });
}

export async function setWorkflowActiveAction(workflowId: string, active: boolean) {
  return runAction(async () => {
    await approvalService.setWorkflowActive(workflowId, active);
    return { id: workflowId };
  });
}

===== .\src\app\(app)\approvals\page.tsx =====
import { Fragment } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { AlarmClock, ArrowRight, ClipboardCheck, Clock, Eye, Send, ShieldCheck, Users, Zap } from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import { StatusBadge } from "@/components/detail/status-badge";
import { Avatar } from "@/components/layout/avatar";
import { EmptyState, Panel } from "@/components/dashboard/widgets";
import { RevealObserver } from "@/components/dashboard/reveal-observer";
import { Hero, StatCard, TypeChip, humanize } from "@/components/approvals/ui";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { can, requireUser } from "@/lib/permissions";
import { delegationCovers } from "@/lib/approval-calc";
import { approvalService } from "@/services/approval.service";
import { ApprovalButtons } from "./approval-buttons";
import { DelegationPanel } from "./delegation-panel";
import { RuleToggle } from "./rule-toggle";

const ENTITY_HREF: Record<string, (id: string) => string> = {
  PurchaseOrder: (id) => `/purchase-orders/${id}`,
  PurchaseRequisition: (id) => `/prs/${id}`,
  CostSheet: (id) => `/costing/${id}`,
  Shipment: (id) => `/shipments/${id}`,
  SampleRequest: (id) => `/samples/${id}`,
};

const TZ = "Asia/Dhaka";
const day = (d: Date) => d.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
const ymd = (d: Date) => d.toLocaleDateString("en-CA", { timeZone: TZ });
const dateFmt = (d: Date) =>
  d.toLocaleDateString("en-US", { month: "short", day: "2-digit", year: "numeric", timeZone: TZ });
const timeFmt = (d: Date) =>
  d.toLocaleTimeString("en-US", { hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit", timeZone: TZ });
const money = (n: unknown) =>
  n === null || n === undefined
    ? "-"
    : new Intl.NumberFormat("en-US", { minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 }).format(Number(n));
const whole = (n: unknown) => new Intl.NumberFormat("en-US", { maximumFractionDigits: 0 }).format(Number(n));

const TH = "px-3 py-2.5 text-left text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600";

export default async function ApprovalsPage() {
  const user = await requireUser();
  const canDecide = can(user, "approval.decide");
  const canRequest = can(user, "approval.request");
  const canManage = can(user, "approval.manage");
  const now = new Date();

  const pending = canDecide ? await approvalService.listPending() : null;
  const mine = canRequest ? await approvalService.listMine() : null;
  const workflows = canManage ? await approvalService.listWorkflows() : null;
  const roles = canManage
    ? await prisma.role.findMany({ select: { id: true, name: true }, orderBy: { name: "asc" } })
    : [];
  const delegation = canDecide ? await approvalService.listDelegations() : null;
  const delegateUsers = canDecide
    ? await prisma.user.findMany({
        where: { companyId: user.companyId, status: "ACTIVE", id: { not: user.id } },
        select: { id: true, name: true, email: true },
        orderBy: { name: "asc" },
      })
    : [];

  const mapDel = (
    list: { id: string; startDate: Date; endDate: Date; reason: string; isActive: boolean }[],
    who: (i: number) => string
  ) =>
    list.map((d, i) => ({
      id: d.id,
      who: who(i),
      start: day(d.startDate),
      end: day(d.endDate),
      reason: d.reason,
      active: d.isActive,
    }));

  const requesterIds = pending ? [...new Set(pending.map((r) => r.requestedById))] : [];
  const requesters = requesterIds.length
    ? await prisma.user.findMany({
        where: { id: { in: requesterIds } },
        select: { id: true, name: true, email: true },
      })
    : [];
  const roleName = (id: string) => roles.find((r) => r.id === id)?.name ?? "-";

  const titleCell = (type: string, id: string, title: string) => {
    const href = ENTITY_HREF[type]?.(id);
    return href ? (
      <Link href={href} className="font-semibold text-navy-800 hover:text-blue-600 hover:underline">
        {title}
      </Link>
    ) : (
      <span className="font-semibold text-navy-800">{title}</span>
    );
  };

  // stat card-er real number
  const pendingCount = pending?.length ?? 0;
  const mineCount = mine?.length ?? 0;
  const minePending = mine?.filter((r) => r.status === "PENDING").length ?? 0;
  const givenActive = delegation?.given.filter((d) => delegationCovers(d, now)).length ?? 0;
  const receivedActive = delegation?.received.filter((d) => delegationCovers(d, now)).length ?? 0;
  const activeRules = workflows?.filter((w) => w.isActive).length ?? 0;

  const nothing = !canDecide && !canRequest && !canManage;

  return (
    <div data-reveal-root className="space-y-5">
      <RevealObserver />
      <Hero
        icon={ShieldCheck}
        title="Approvals"
        subtitle="Manage approval workflows and keep track of pending requests"
      />

      {nothing && <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Your role has no approval permissions.</p>}

      {/* ---------- Stat cards ---------- */}
      {!nothing && (
        <div className="stagger grid grid-cols-[repeat(auto-fit,minmax(230px,1fr))] gap-4">
          {pending && (
            <StatCard theme="blue" icon={Clock} label="Pending Approvals" value={pendingCount} sub="Need your decision" />
          )}
          {mine && (
            <StatCard theme="green" icon={Send} label="My Requests" value={mineCount} sub={`${minePending} still pending`} />
          )}
          {delegation && (
            <StatCard
              theme="violet"
              icon={Users}
              label="Delegated"
              value={givenActive}
              sub={`${receivedActive} delegated to you`}
            />
          )}
          {workflows && (
            <StatCard
              theme="orange"
              icon={ShieldCheck}
              label="Active Rules"
              value={activeRules}
              sub={`${workflows.length} rule(s) in total`}
            />
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* ---------- Waiting for me ---------- */}
      {pending && (
        <Panel
          icon={ClipboardCheck}
          iconClass="bg-blue-100 text-blue-600"
          title="Waiting for me"
          subtitle="Requests that need your decision"
          aside={
            <span className="rounded-full bg-blue-100 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-blue-700">
              {pendingCount}
            </span>
          }
        >
          {pending.length === 0 ? (
            <EmptyState>Nothing waiting for you</EmptyState>
          ) : (
            <div className="overflow-x-auto rounded-xl">
              <table className="w-full min-w-[860px] text-sm">
                <thead>
                  <tr className="bg-blue-50/70">
                    <th className={`${TH} rounded-l-lg`}>ID</th>
                    <th className={TH}>Type</th>
                    <th className={TH}>Request details</th>
                    <th className={`${TH} text-right`}>Amount</th>
                    <th className={TH}>Requested by</th>
                    <th className={TH}>Date</th>
                    <th className={`${TH} rounded-r-lg text-right`}>Action</th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody className="stagger stagger-fast">
                  {pending.map((r) => {
                    const who = requesters.find((u) => u.id === r.requestedById);
                    const whoName = who?.name ?? who?.email ?? "-";
                    const step = r.workflow?.steps.find((s) => s.stepOrder === r.currentStep);
                    return (
                      <tr key={r.id} className="border-b border-slate-100 last:border-0">
                        <td className="px-3 py-3 text-slate-500">#{r.id.slice(-6).toUpperCase()}</td>
                        <td className="px-3 py-3">
                          <TypeChip type={r.entityType} />
                        </td>
                        <td className="px-3 py-3">
                          {titleCell(r.entityType, r.entityId, r.title)}
                          {step && <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500">Step: {step.name}</p>}
                        </td>
                        <td className="px-3 py-3 text-right font-medium text-navy-800">{money(r.amount)}</td>
                        <td className="px-3 py-3">
                          <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
                            <Avatar name={whoName} className="h-7 w-7 text-[10px]" />
                            {whoName}
                          </span>
                        </td>
                        <td className="whitespace-nowrap px-3 py-3 text-slate-600">
                          {dateFmt(r.requestedAt)} <span className="text-slate-400">â€¢</span> {timeFmt(r.requestedAt)}
                        </td>
                        <td className="px-3 py-3 text-right">
                          <Link
                            href={`/approvals/${r.id}`}
                            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full bg-blue-50 px-4 py-1.5 text-sm font-semibold text-blue-600 transition-colors hover:bg-blue-100"
                          >
                            <Eye className="h-4 w-4" />
                            Review
                          </Link>
                        </td>
                      </tr>
                    );
                  })}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          )}
        </Panel>
      )}

      {/* ---------- My requests ---------- */}
      {mine && (
        <Panel
          icon={Send}
          iconClass="bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-600"
          title="My requests"
          subtitle="Requests you have sent for approval"
        >
          {mine.length === 0 ? (
            <EmptyState>You have not sent any request</EmptyState>
          ) : (
            <div className="overflow-x-auto rounded-xl">
              <table className="w-full min-w-[720px] text-sm">

                  <tr className="bg-blue-50/70">
                    <th className={`${TH} rounded-l-lg`}>Request</th>

                    <th className={`${TH} text-right`}>Amount</th>
                    <th className={TH}>Status</th>
                    <th className={TH}>Date</th>
                    <th className={`${TH} rounded-r-lg`} />
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody className="stagger stagger-fast">
                  {mine.map((r) => (
                    <tr key={r.id} className="border-b border-slate-100 last:border-0">
                      <td className="px-3 py-3">{titleCell(r.entityType, r.entityId, r.title)}</td>
                      <td className="px-3 py-3">
                        <TypeChip type={r.entityType} />
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-3 py-3 text-right font-medium text-navy-800">{money(r.amount)}</td>
                      <td className="px-3 py-3">
                        <StatusBadge status={r.status} />
                      </td>
                      <td className="whitespace-nowrap px-3 py-3 text-slate-600">{dateFmt(r.requestedAt)}</td>
                      <td className="px-3 py-3">{r.status === "PENDING" && <ApprovalButtons id={r.id} mode="cancel" />}</td>
                    </tr>
                  ))}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          )}
        </Panel>
      )}

      {/* ---------- Delegation + Escalation ---------- */}
      {(delegation || canManage) && (

          {delegation && (

              users={delegateUsers.map((u) => ({ id: u.id, label: `${u.name} (${u.email})` }))}
              given={mapDel(delegation.given, (i) => delegation.given[i].toUser.name)}
              received={mapDel(delegation.received, (i) => delegation.received[i].fromUser.name)}
              defaultStart={ymd(now)}
              defaultEnd={ymd(new Date(now.getTime() + 7 * 86_400_000))}
            />
          )}

          {canManage && (
            <section className="relative flex flex-col justify-between overflow-hidden rounded-card border bg-linear-to-br from-indigo-50 via-blue-50 to-white p-5 shadow-card">
              <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
                <span className="flex h-10 w-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-600">
                  <Zap className="h-5 w-5" />
                </span>
                <div>
                  <h2 className="font-heading text-base font-semibold">Escalation</h2>
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">Set up escalation rules for pending approvals</p>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="mt-6 flex items-end justify-between gap-3">
                <Link
                  href="/approvals/rules#escalation"
                  className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg border border-indigo-200 bg-white px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-indigo-700 transition-colors hover:bg-indigo-50"

                <AlarmClock className="h-20 w-20 shrink-0 text-indigo-400/70" strokeWidth={1.5} />
              </div>
            </section>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* ---------- Approval rules ---------- */}
      {workflows && (
        <Panel
          icon={ShieldCheck}
          iconClass="bg-blue-100 text-blue-600"
          title="Approval rules"
          subtitle="Manage your approval workflows and rules"
          aside={
            <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-3">
              <span className="rounded-full bg-emerald-100 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-emerald-700">
                {activeRules} Active
              </span>
              <Link
                href="/approvals/rules"
                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg border bg-card px-3.5 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-navy-800 transition-colors hover:bg-slate-50"
              >
                Manage Rules <ArrowRight className="h-4 w-4" />
              </Link>
            </div>
          }
        >
          {workflows.length === 0 ? (
            <EmptyState>
              No rules yet. Records are approved without a workflow
              <Link href="/approvals/rules" className="rounded-md bg-primary px-3 py-1.5 text-sm text-primary-foreground">
                Add first rule
              </Link>
            </EmptyState>
          ) : (
            <div className="overflow-x-auto rounded-xl">
              <table className="w-full min-w-[760px] text-sm">
                <thead>
                  <tr className="bg-blue-50/70">
                    <th className={`${TH} rounded-l-lg`}>Code</th>
                    <th className={TH}>Name</th>
                    <th className={TH}>Applies to</th>
                    <th className={TH}>Amount range</th>
                    <th className={TH}>Steps</th>
                    <th className={`${TH} rounded-r-lg`}>Active</th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody className="stagger stagger-fast">
                  {workflows.map((w) => (
                    <tr key={w.id} className="border-b border-slate-100 last:border-0">
                      <td className="px-3 py-3 font-medium text-slate-600">{w.code}</td>
                      <td className="px-3 py-3 font-semibold text-navy-800">{w.name}</td>
                      <td className="px-3 py-3">{humanize(w.entityType)}</td>
                      <td className="whitespace-nowrap px-3 py-3 text-slate-700">
                        {w.minAmount === null ? "0" : whole(w.minAmount)} â€“ {w.maxAmount === null ? "No limit" : whole(w.maxAmount)}
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-3 py-3">
                        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5">
                          {w.steps.map((s, i) => (
                            <Fragment key={s.id}>
                              {i > 0 && <ArrowRight className="h-3 w-3 text-slate-400" />}
                              <span
                                title={`${s.name} (${roleName(s.approverRoleId)})`}
                                className="flex h-6 w-6 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-blue-100 text-xs font-semibold text-blue-700"
                              >
                                {s.stepOrder}
                              </span>
                            </Fragment>
                          ))}
                        </span>
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-3 py-3">
                        <RuleToggle key={`${w.id}-${w.isActive}`} id={w.id} active={w.isActive} name={w.name} />
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  ))}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          )}
        </Panel>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

===== .\src\app\(app)\approvals\request-actions.ts =====
"use server";

import { runAction } from "@/lib/action-result";
import { approvalService } from "@/services/approval.service";

export async function requestApprovalAction(v: { entityType: string; entityId: string; title: string; amount?: number }) {
  return runAction(async () => {
    const row = await approvalService.submit(v);
    return { id: row.id };
  });
}

===== .\src\app\(app)\approvals\request-approval-button.tsx =====
"use client";

import { useState, useTransition } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { requestApprovalAction } from "./request-actions";

export function RequestApprovalButton(props: { entityType: string; entityId: string; title: string; amount?: number }) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [msg, setMsg] = useState("");
  const [pending, start] = useTransition();

  function go() {
    setMsg("");
    start(async () => {
      const res = await requestApprovalAction(props);
      if (!res.ok) setMsg(res.error ?? "Failed");
      else router.push(`/approvals/${res.data.id}`);
    });
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-1">
      <Button size="sm" variant="outline" disabled={pending} onClick={go}>Request approval</Button>
      {msg && <p className="text-xs text-destructive">{msg}</p>}
    </div>
  );
}

===== .\src\app\(app)\approvals\rule-toggle.tsx =====
"use client";

import { useState, useTransition } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { Switch } from "@/components/approvals/switch";
import { setWorkflowActiveAction } from "./extra-actions";

export function RuleToggle({ id, active, name }: { id: string; active: boolean; name: string }) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [on, setOn] = useState(active);
  const [err, setErr] = useState("");
  const [pending, start] = useTransition();

  function change(next: boolean) {
    setOn(next);
    setErr("");
    start(async () => {
      const res = await setWorkflowActiveAction(id, next);
      if (!res.ok) {
        setOn(!next);
        setErr(res.error);
      } else {
        router.refresh();
      }
    });
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
      <Switch checked={on} onChange={change} disabled={pending} label={`${name} active`} />
      {err && <span className="text-xs text-destructive">{err}</span>}
    </div>
  );
}

===== .\src\app\(app)\approvals\who-is-pending.tsx =====
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { approvalService } from "@/services/approval.service";

export async function WhoIsPending({ id }: { id: string }) {
  const d = await approvalService.requestDetail(id);
  return (
    <Card>
      <CardHeader>
        <CardTitle>Approval progress</CardTitle>
      </CardHeader>
      <CardContent className="space-y-2 text-sm">
        <ol className="space-y-1">
          {d.steps.map((s) => {
            const act = d.req.actions.find((a) => a.stepOrder === s.stepOrder);
            return (
              <li key={s.id}>
                {s.stepOrder}. {s.name} ({s.approverRole.name}):{" "}
                {act
                  ? `${act.decision.toLowerCase()} by ${act.approver.name}${act.delegatedFromId ? " (as delegate)" : ""}`
                  : s.stepOrder === d.req.currentStep && d.req.status === "PENDING"
                    ? "waiting"
                    : "not reached"}
              </li>
            );
          })}
        </ol>
        {d.req.status === "PENDING" && (
          <div>
            <p className="font-medium">Waiting on ({d.pendingDays} day(s) at this step)</p>
            {d.waitingOn.length === 0 && <p className="text-muted-foreground">No active user holds this role</p>}
            {d.waitingOn.map((u) => (
              <p key={`${u.id}-${u.via ?? ""}`}>{u.name} ({u.email}){u.via ? ` - ${u.via}` : ""}</p>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
        {d.req.escalations.length > 0 && <p className="text-muted-foreground">Escalated {d.req.escalations.length} time(s)</p>}
      </CardContent>
    </Card>
  );
}

===== .\src\app\(app)\notifications\notification-list.tsx =====
"use client";

import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useState, useTransition } from "react";
import { markAllNotificationsReadAction, markNotificationReadAction } from "@/app/(app)/actions";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

type Item = {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  message: string;
  href: string | null;
  read: boolean;
  createdAt: string;
};

export function NotificationList({ items }: { items: Item[] }) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [list, setList] = useState(items);
  const [, start] = useTransition();
  const unread = list.filter((n) => !n.read).length;

  function open(n: Item) {
    if (!n.read) {
      setList((l) => l.map((x) => (x.id === n.id ? { ...x, read: true } : x)));
      start(async () => {
        await markNotificationReadAction(n.id);
      });
    }
    if (n.href) router.push(n.href);
  }

  function readAll() {
    setList((l) => l.map((x) => ({ ...x, read: true })));
    start(async () => {
      await markAllNotificationsReadAction();
    });
  }

  return (
    <div>
      {unread > 0 && (
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b px-4 py-2 text-sm">
          <span className="text-muted-foreground">{unread} unread</span>
          <button onClick={readAll} className="text-denim-700 hover:underline">
            Mark all read
          </button>
        </div>
      )}
      {list.length === 0 ? (
        <p className="p-6 text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground">No notifications yet</p>
      ) : (
        <ul>
          {list.map((n) => (
            <li key={n.id}>
              <button
                onClick={() => open(n)}
                className={cn("block w-full border-b px-4 py-2.5 text-left hover:bg-muted", !n.read && "bg-denim-100/40")}
              >
                <p className={cn("text-sm", !n.read && "font-semibold")}>{n.title}</p>
                <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">{n.message}</p>
                <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-muted-foreground">{new Date(n.createdAt).toLocaleString()}</p>
              </button>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

===== .\src\app\(app)\notifications\page.tsx =====
import { PageHeader } from "@/components/layout/page-header";
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { NotificationType } from "@/generated/prisma/enums";
import { notificationService } from "@/services/notification.service";
import { NotificationList } from "./notification-list";
import { PreferencesForm } from "./preferences-form";

export default async function NotificationsPage() {
  const [list, prefs] = await Promise.all([notificationService.list(false, 100), notificationService.getPreferences()]);

  const items = list.map((n) => ({
    id: n.id,
    title: n.title,
    message: n.message,
    href: n.href ?? null,
    read: n.readAt !== null,
    createdAt: n.createdAt.toISOString(),
  }));

  // à¦¸à§‡à¦­ à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦ªà¦›à¦¨à§à¦¦ à¦¨à¦¾ à¦¥à¦¾à¦•à¦²à§‡ à¦¡à¦¿à¦«à¦²à§à¦Ÿ: In-app à¦šà¦¾à¦²à§, Email à¦¬à¦¨à§à¦§ (notify() à¦à¦° à¦¨à¦¿à¦¯à¦¼à¦®à§‡à¦° à¦¸à¦¾à¦¥à§‡ à¦®à¦¿à¦²)
  const rows = Object.values(NotificationType).map((type) => {
    const p = prefs.find((x) => x.type === type);
    return { type, inApp: p?.inApp ?? true, email: p?.email ?? false };
  });

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <PageHeader title="Notifications" description="Your latest messages and how you want to receive them" />
      <div className="grid gap-4 lg:grid-cols-3">
        <Card className="lg:col-span-2">
          <CardHeader>
            <CardTitle>Latest 100</CardTitle>
          </CardHeader>
          <CardContent className="p-0">
            <NotificationList items={items} />
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
        <Card>
          <CardHeader>
            <CardTitle>Preferences</CardTitle>
          </CardHeader>
          <CardContent>
            <PreferencesForm rows={rows} />
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== .\src\app\(app)\notifications\preferences-form.tsx =====
"use client";

import { useState, useTransition } from "react";
import { setNotificationPreferenceAction } from "@/app/(app)/actions";

type Row = { type: string; inApp: boolean; email: boolean };

const label = (t: string) => t.replace(/_/g, " ").toLowerCase().replace(/^\w/, (c) => c.toUpperCase());

// à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¤à¦¿à¦Ÿà¦¿ à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦• à¦¦à¦¿à¦²à§‡à¦‡ à¦¸à¦¾à¦¥à§‡ à¦¸à¦¾à¦¥à§‡ à¦¸à§‡à¦­ à¦¹à¦¯à¦¼; à¦¬à§à¦¯à¦°à§à¦¥ à¦¹à¦²à§‡ à¦†à¦—à§‡à¦° à¦…à¦¬à¦¸à§à¦¥à¦¾à¦¯à¦¼ à¦«à§‡à¦°à§‡
export function PreferencesForm({ rows }: { rows: Row[] }) {
  const [state, setState] = useState(rows);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [pending, start] = useTransition();

  function toggle(type: string, field: "inApp" | "email") {
    const prev = state;
    const cur = state.find((r) => r.type === type)!;
    const next = { ...cur, [field]: !cur[field] };
    setState(state.map((r) => (r.type === type ? next : r)));
    setError("");
    start(async () => {
      const res = await setNotificationPreferenceAction(type, next.inApp, next.email);
      if (!res.ok) {
        setState(prev);
        setError(res.error);
      }
    });
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-2">
      <div className="grid grid-cols-[1fr_auto_auto] items-center gap-x-4 gap-y-2 text-sm">
        <span />
        <span className="text-xs font-medium uppercase text-muted-foreground">In-app</span>
        <span className="text-xs font-medium uppercase text-muted-foreground">Email</span>
        {state.map((r) => (
          <div key={r.type} className="contents">
            <span>{label(r.type)}</span>
            <input
              type="checkbox"
              checked={r.inApp}
              disabled={pending}
              onChange={() => toggle(r.type, "inApp")}
              aria-label={`${label(r.type)} in-app`}
            />
            <input
              type="checkbox"
              checked={r.email}
              disabled={pending}
              onChange={() => toggle(r.type, "email")}
              aria-label={`${label(r.type)} email`}
            />
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
      {error && <p className="text-sm text-destructive">{error}</p>}
      <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Email messages are queued now and will be sent once email delivery is connected.</p>
    </div>
  );
}

===== .\src\app\(app)\layout.tsx =====
import { redirect } from "next/navigation";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { AuthError, can, requireUser } from "@/lib/permissions";
import { listSwitchOptions } from "@/services/context.service";
import { notificationService } from "@/services/notification.service";
import { AppShell } from "@/components/layout/app-shell";
import { NAV, type NavGroupData } from "@/components/layout/nav-config";
import { isTextileEnabled } from "@/lib/textile-flag";
import { getAvatarUrl } from "@/lib/avatar";

export default async function AppLayout({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  // Only "not logged in" (AuthError) goes to /login. Any other error is shown, not hidden.
  const user = await requireUser().catch((e) => {
    if (e instanceof AuthError) return null;
    throw e;
  });
  if (!user) redirect("/login");

  const [company, companies, unreadCount, recent, me, role, avatar] = await Promise.all([
    prisma.company.findUnique({
      where: { id: user.companyId },
      select: { tradeName: true, legalName: true },
    }),
    listSwitchOptions(user.id),
    notificationService.unreadCount(),
    notificationService.list(false, 8),
    prisma.user.findUnique({ where: { id: user.id }, select: { name: true } }),
    prisma.role.findUnique({ where: { id: user.roleId }, select: { name: true } }),
    getAvatarUrl(user.id),
  ]);
  const textileEnabled = await isTextileEnabled(user.id);
  // à¦¯à§‡ permission à¦¨à§‡à¦‡, à¦¸à§‡à¦‡ à¦®à§‡à¦¨à§ à¦¦à§‡à¦–à¦¾à¦¨à§‹ à¦¹à¦¯à¦¼ à¦¨à¦¾
    const groups: NavGroupData[] = NAV.filter((g) => g.feature !== "textile" || textileEnabled).map((g) => ({
    label: g.label,
    items: g.items.filter((i) => !i.permission || can(user, i.permission)),
  })).filter((g) => g.items.length > 0);

  return (
    <AppShell
      groups={groups}
      companyName={company?.tradeName ?? company?.legalName ?? "-"}
      companies={companies.map((c) => ({ id: c.id, name: c.name }))}
      activeCompanyId={user.companyId}
      userEmail={user.email}
      userName={me?.name ?? user.email}
      userRole={role?.name ?? ""}
      avatarUrl={avatar}
      unreadCount={unreadCount}
      notifications={recent.map((n) => ({
        id: n.id,
        title: n.title,
        message: n.message,
        href: n.href ?? null,
        read: n.readAt !== null,
        createdAt: n.createdAt.toISOString(),
      }))}
    >
      {children}
    </AppShell>
  );
}

===== .\src\app\(portal)\portal\layout.tsx =====
import { redirect } from "next/navigation";
import { auth, signOut } from "@/lib/auth";

export default async function PortalLayout({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const session = await auth();
  const type = session?.user?.portalType;
  if (!type) redirect("/login");

  async function logout() {
    "use server";
    await signOut({ redirectTo: "/login" });
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-gray-50">
      <header className="flex items-center justify-between border-b bg-white px-6 py-3">
        <div className="font-semibold">
          MERCHFLOW {type === "SUPPLIER" ? "Supplier" : "Buyer"} Portal
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-4 text-sm">
          <span className="text-gray-600">{session?.user?.email}</span>
          <form action={logout}>
            <button type="submit" className="rounded border px-3 py-1 hover:bg-gray-100">
              Sign out
            </button>
          </form>
        </div>
      </header>
      <main className="mx-auto max-w-6xl p-6">{children}</main>
    </div>
  );
}

===== .\src\components\approvals\switch.tsx =====
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

export function Switch({
  checked,
  onChange,
  disabled,
  label,
}: {
  checked: boolean;
  onChange: (next: boolean) => void;
  disabled?: boolean;
  label: string;
}) {
  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      role="switch"
      aria-checked={checked}
      aria-label={label}
      disabled={disabled}
      onClick={() => onChange(!checked)}
      className={cn(
        "relative inline-flex h-6 w-11 shrink-0 items-center rounded-full transition-colors disabled:opacity-60",
        checked ? "bg-blue-600" : "bg-slate-300"
      )}
    >
      <span
        className={cn(
          "inline-block h-5 w-5 rounded-full bg-white shadow transition-transform",
          checked ? "translate-x-5.5" : "translate-x-0.5"
        )}
      />
    </button>
  );
}

import { Check, ShieldCheck, type LucideIcon } from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import { AnimatedValue } from "@/components/dashboard/animated-value";

export const humanize = (s: string) => s.replace(/([a-z])([A-Z])/g, "$1 $2");

const TYPE_STYLE: Record<string, string> = {
  PurchaseOrder: "bg-blue-100 text-blue-700",
  PurchaseRequisition: "bg-violet-100 text-violet-700",
  CostSheet: "bg-teal-100 text-teal-700",
  Shipment: "bg-sky-100 text-sky-700",
  SampleRequest: "bg-pink-100 text-pink-700",
  MaterialIssue: "bg-amber-100 text-amber-700",
  StockAdjustment: "bg-orange-100 text-orange-700",
  CreditNote: "bg-rose-100 text-rose-700",
};

export function TypeChip({ type }: { type: string }) {
  return (
    <span
      className={cn(
        "inline-block whitespace-nowrap rounded-full px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold",
        TYPE_STYLE[type] ?? "bg-slate-100 text-slate-700"
      )}
    >
      {humanize(type)}
    </span>
  );
}

export function Hero({ icon: Icon, title, subtitle }: { icon: LucideIcon; title: string; subtitle: string }) {

    <header className="relative overflow-hidden rounded-card border bg-linear-to-r from-blue-50 via-white to-blue-50/60 p-5 shadow-card">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
          <span className="flex h-16 w-16 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl border border-blue-200 bg-white text-blue-600 shadow-card">
            <Icon className="h-8 w-8" fill="currentColor" fillOpacity={0.15} />
          </span>
          <div>
            <h1 className="font-heading text-3xl font-semibold text-navy-800">{title}</h1>
            <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">{subtitle}</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        {/* decoration */}
        <div aria-hidden="true" className="relative mr-4 hidden h-20 w-44 md:block">
          <div className="absolute right-10 top-0 h-[4.5rem] w-14 -rotate-6 space-y-2 rounded-xl border bg-white p-3 shadow-lg">
            <span className="block h-1.5 w-full rounded bg-blue-200" />
            <span className="block h-1.5 w-3/4 rounded bg-slate-200" />
            <span className="block h-1.5 w-full rounded bg-slate-200" />
          </div>
          <span className="absolute bottom-0 right-4 flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-lg ring-4 ring-white">
            <Check className="h-5 w-5" strokeWidth={3} />
          </span>
          <ShieldCheck className="absolute right-0 top-1 h-6 w-6 text-blue-300" />
        </div>
      </div>
    </header>
  );
}

const STAT = {
  blue: { card: "border-blue-200/70 from-blue-100/70 via-blue-50 to-white", icon: "bg-blue-100 text-blue-600" },
  green: { card: "border-emerald-200/70 from-emerald-100/70 via-emerald-50 to-white", icon: "bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-600" },
  violet: { card: "border-violet-200/70 from-violet-100/70 via-violet-50 to-white", icon: "bg-violet-100 text-violet-600" },
  orange: { card: "border-orange-200/70 from-orange-100/70 via-orange-50 to-white", icon: "bg-orange-100 text-orange-600" },
};

export function StatCard({
  icon: Icon,
  label,
  value,
  sub,
  theme,
}: {
  icon: LucideIcon;
  label: string;
  value: number;
  sub: string;
  theme: keyof typeof STAT;
}) {
  const T = STAT[theme];
  return (
    <div
      className={cn(
        "group flex items-center gap-4 rounded-card border bg-linear-to-br p-5 shadow-card transition-all duration-200 hover:-translate-y-1 hover:shadow-lg",
        T.card
      )}
    >
      <span
        className={cn(
          "flex h-14 w-14 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full transition-transform duration-200 group-hover:scale-110",
          T.icon
        )}
      >
        <Icon className="h-6 w-6" strokeWidth={2.2} />
      </span>
      <div className="min-w-0">
        <p className="text-sm font-medium text-navy-800">{label}</p>
        <p className="text-3xl font-bold leading-tight tabular-nums text-navy-800">
          <AnimatedValue value={value} />
        </p>
        <p className="truncate text-xs text-slate-500">{sub}</p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";

export function AppShell({
  groups,
  companyName,
  companies,
  activeCompanyId,
  userEmail,
  userName,
  userRole,
  avatarUrl,
  unreadCount,
  notifications,
  children,
}: {
  groups: NavGroupData[];
  companyName: string;
  companies: { id: string; name: string }[];
  activeCompanyId: string;
  userEmail: string;
  userName: string;
  userRole: string;
  avatarUrl: string | null;
  unreadCount: number;
  notifications: BellItem[];

}) {

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen">
      <Sidebar groups={groups} open={open} onClose={() => setOpen(false)} />

          onMenu={() => setOpen(true)}
          companyName={companyName}
          companies={companies}
          activeCompanyId={activeCompanyId}
          userEmail={userEmail}
          userName={userName}
          userRole={userRole}

      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== .\src\components\layout\avatar.tsx =====
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

const initials = (name: string) =>
  name
    .split(/\s+/)
    .filter(Boolean)
    .slice(0, 2)
    .map((p) => p[0]?.toUpperCase())
    .join("") || "?";

// gol profile chhobi; chhobi na thakle nam-er prothom okkhor
export function Avatar({ name, src, className }: { name: string; src?: string | null; className?: string }) {
  return src ? (
    // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
    <img
      src={src}
      alt={name}
      className={cn("h-10 w-10 shrink-0 rounded-full object-cover shadow ring-2 ring-white", className)}
    />
  ) : (
    <span
      aria-label={name}
      className={cn(
        "flex h-10 w-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-linear-to-br from-blue-600 to-sky-400 text-sm font-semibold text-white shadow ring-2 ring-white",
        className
      )}
    >
      {initials(name)}
    </span>
  );
}
