===== .\src\components\layout\notification-bell.tsx =====
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useEffect, useRef, useState, useTransition } from "react";
import { Bell } from "lucide-react";
import { markAllNotificationsReadAction, markNotificationReadAction } from "@/app/(app)/actions";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

export type BellItem = {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  message: string;
  href: string | null;
  read: boolean;
  createdAt: string; // ISO
};

// à¦Ÿà¦ªà¦¬à¦¾à¦°à§‡à¦° à¦˜à¦£à§à¦Ÿà¦¾: à¦…à¦ªà¦ à¦¿à¦¤ à¦¸à¦‚à¦–à§à¦¯à¦¾ + à¦¸à¦°à§à¦¬à¦¶à§‡à¦· à§®à¦Ÿà¦¿à¥¤ à¦¡à§à¦°à¦ªà¦¡à¦¾à¦‰à¦¨ à¦–à§à¦²à¦²à§‡à¦‡ à¦¶à§à¦§à§ à¦¤à¦¾à¦°à¦¿à¦– à¦†à¦à¦•à¦¾ à¦¹à¦¯à¦¼ (hydration mismatch à¦à¦¡à¦¼à¦¾à¦¤à§‡)
// à¦¸à¦¾à¦°à§à¦­à¦¾à¦° à¦¥à§‡à¦•à§‡ à¦¨à¦¤à§à¦¨ à¦¡à¦¾à¦Ÿà¦¾ à¦à¦²à§‡ topbar.tsx-à¦à¦° key à¦¬à¦¦à¦²à§‡ à¦•à¦®à§à¦ªà§‹à¦¨à§‡à¦¨à§à¦Ÿ à¦¨à¦¤à§à¦¨ à¦•à¦°à§‡ à¦¶à§à¦°à§ à¦¹à¦¯à¦¼, à¦¤à¦¾à¦‡ à¦à¦–à¦¾à¦¨à§‡ sync effect à¦²à¦¾à¦—à§‡ à¦¨à¦¾
export function NotificationBell({ unreadCount, items }: { unreadCount: number; items: BellItem[] }) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [list, setList] = useState(items);
  const [unread, setUnread] = useState(unreadCount);
  const [, start] = useTransition();
  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    function onDown(e: MouseEvent) {
      if (ref.current && !ref.current.contains(e.target as Node)) setOpen(false);
    }
    document.addEventListener("mousedown", onDown);
    return () => document.removeEventListener("mousedown", onDown);
  }, []);

  function openItem(n: BellItem) {
    setOpen(false);
    if (!n.read) {
      setList((l) => l.map((x) => (x.id === n.id ? { ...x, read: true } : x)));
      setUnread((u) => Math.max(0, u - 1));
      start(async () => {
        await markNotificationReadAction(n.id);
      });
    }
    if (n.href) router.push(n.href);
  }

  function readAll() {
    setList((l) => l.map((x) => ({ ...x, read: true })));
    setUnread(0);
    start(async () => {
      await markAllNotificationsReadAction();
    });
  }

  return (
    <div ref={ref} className="relative">
      <button
        onClick={() => setOpen((o) => !o)}
        className="relative rounded-md border p-1.5 hover:bg-muted"
        aria-label={`Notifications, ${unread} unread`}
      >
        <Bell className="h-4 w-4" />
        {unread > 0 && (
          <span className="absolute -right-1.5 -top-1.5 min-w-4 rounded-full bg-destructive px-1 text-center text-[10px] font-semibold leading-4 text-white">
            {unread > 99 ? "99+" : unread}
          </span>
        )}
      </button>

      {open && (
        <div className="absolute right-0 top-10 z-30 w-80 rounded-md border bg-card shadow-lg">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b px-3 py-2">
            <span className="text-sm font-semibold">Notifications</span>
            {unread > 0 && (
              <button onClick={readAll} className="text-xs text-denim-700 hover:underline">
                Mark all read
              </button>
            )}
          </div>

          <ul className="max-h-96 overflow-y-auto">
            {list.length === 0 && <li className="p-4 text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground">Nothing yet</li>}
            {list.map((n) => (
              <li key={n.id}>
                <button
                  onClick={() => openItem(n)}
                  className={cn("block w-full border-b px-3 py-2 text-left hover:bg-muted", !n.read && "bg-denim-100/40")}
                >
                  <p className={cn("text-sm", !n.read && "font-semibold")}>{n.title}</p>
                  <p className="line-clamp-2 text-xs text-muted-foreground">{n.message}</p>
                  <p className="mt-0.5 text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">
                    {new Date(n.createdAt).toLocaleString()}
                  </p>
                </button>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>

          <Link
            href="/notifications"
            onClick={() => setOpen(false)}
            className="block px-3 py-2 text-center text-sm text-denim-700 hover:bg-muted"
          >
            See all and preferences
          </Link>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

===== .\src\components\layout\page-header.tsx =====
export function PageHeader({
  title,
  description,
  actions,
}: {
  title: string;
  description?: string;
  actions?: React.ReactNode;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-3">
      <div>
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold">{title}</h1>
        {description && <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">{description}</p>}
      </div>
      {actions && <div className="flex gap-2">{actions}</div>}
    </div>
  );
}

===== .\src\components\layout\search-box.tsx =====
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { Search } from "lucide-react";
import { searchAction } from "@/app/(app)/actions";

type Hit = { type: string; id: string; label: string; href: string };

// à¦Ÿà¦ªà¦¬à¦¾à¦°à§‡à¦° à¦—à§à¦²à§‹à¦¬à¦¾à¦² à¦¸à¦¾à¦°à§à¦š: à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦‡à¦ª à¦¥à¦¾à¦®à¦¾à¦° à§©à§¦à§¦ms à¦ªà¦°à§‡ à¦–à§‹à¦à¦œà§‡, à¦¬à¦¾à¦‡à¦°à§‡ à¦•à§à¦²à¦¿à¦• à¦•à¦°à¦²à§‡ à¦¬à¦¨à§à¦§ à¦¹à¦¯à¦¼
export function SearchBox() {
  const [q, setQ] = useState("");
  const [hits, setHits] = useState<Hit[]>([]);
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const boxRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const seq = useRef(0); // à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¨à§‹ à¦‰à¦¤à§à¦¤à¦° à¦¨à¦¤à§à¦¨ à¦‰à¦¤à§à¦¤à¦°à§‡à¦° à¦“à¦ªà¦° à¦¬à¦¸à¦¾ à¦ à§‡à¦•à¦¾à¦¯à¦¼
  const timer = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);

  // à¦¸à¦¾à¦°à§à¦š à¦šà¦¾à¦²à§ à¦¹à¦¯à¦¼ à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦‡à¦ª à¦•à¦°à¦¾à¦° à¦®à§à¦¹à§‚à¦°à§à¦¤à§‡ (effect-à¦ à¦¨à¦¯à¦¼), à¦¤à¦¾à¦‡ cascading render à¦¹à¦¯à¦¼ à¦¨à¦¾
  function onChange(value: string) {
    setQ(value);
    setOpen(true);
    if (timer.current) clearTimeout(timer.current);

    const term = value.trim();
    if (term.length < 2) {
      seq.current++; // à¦šà¦²à¦®à¦¾à¦¨ à¦¸à¦¾à¦°à§à¦šà§‡à¦° à¦‰à¦¤à§à¦¤à¦° à¦¬à¦¾à¦¤à¦¿à¦²
      return;
    }

    const mine = ++seq.current;
    setBusy(true);
    timer.current = setTimeout(async () => {
      const res = await searchAction(term);
      if (mine !== seq.current) return;
      setBusy(false);
      if (res.ok) {
        setHits(res.data);
        setError("");
      } else {
        setHits([]);
        setError(res.error);
      }
    }, 300);
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    function onDown(e: MouseEvent) {
      if (boxRef.current && !boxRef.current.contains(e.target as Node)) setOpen(false);
    }
    document.addEventListener("mousedown", onDown);
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener("mousedown", onDown);
      if (timer.current) clearTimeout(timer.current);
    };
  }, []);

  // à§¨ à¦…à¦•à§à¦·à¦°à§‡à¦° à¦•à¦® à¦¹à¦²à§‡ à¦†à¦—à§‡à¦° à¦«à¦²à¦¾à¦«à¦² à¦¦à§‡à¦–à¦¾à¦¨à§‹ à¦¹à¦¯à¦¼ à¦¨à¦¾ (state à¦¬à¦¦à¦²à¦¾à¦¨à§‹ à¦›à¦¾à¦¡à¦¼à¦¾à¦‡)
  const active = q.trim().length >= 2;
  const shownHits = active ? hits : [];
  const shownBusy = active && busy;
  const shownError = active ? error : "";

  // à¦§à¦°à¦¨ à¦…à¦¨à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦¯à¦¼à§€ à¦¸à¦¾à¦œà¦¾à¦¨à§‹ (Buyer, Order, Style...)
  const types = Array.from(new Set(shownHits.map((h) => h.type)));
  const showPanel = open && active;

  return (
    <div ref={boxRef} className="relative hidden w-72 sm:block">
      <Search className="pointer-events-none absolute left-2.5 top-2 h-4 w-4 text-muted-foreground" />
      <input
        value={q}
        onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)}
        onFocus={() => setOpen(true)}
        onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === "Escape" && setOpen(false)}
        placeholder="Search buyer, order, style, PO..."
        className="h-8 w-full rounded-md border bg-card pl-8 pr-2 text-sm outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-denim-200"
        aria-label="Global search"
      />

      {showPanel && (
        <div className="absolute left-0 top-10 z-30 max-h-96 w-full min-w-80 overflow-y-auto rounded-md border bg-card shadow-lg">
          {shownBusy && shownHits.length === 0 && <p className="p-3 text-sm text-muted-foreground">Searching...</p>}
          {shownError && <p className="p-3 text-sm text-destructive">{shownError}</p>}
          {!shownBusy && !shownError && shownHits.length === 0 && (
            <p className="p-3 text-sm text-muted-foreground">No results for â€œ{q.trim()}â€</p>
          )}
          {types.map((type) => (
            <div key={type}>
              <p className="bg-muted px-3 py-1 text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-muted-foreground">
                {type}
              </p>
              <ul>
                {shownHits
                  .filter((h) => h.type === type)
                  .map((h) => (
                    <li key={`${h.type}:${h.id}`}>
                      <Link
                        href={h.href}
                        onClick={() => setOpen(false)}
                        className="block px-3 py-1.5 text-sm hover:bg-muted"
                      >
                        {h.label}
                      </Link>
                    </li>
                  ))}
              </ul>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

===== .\src\components\layout\sidebar.tsx =====
"use client";

import { createElement, useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { usePathname } from "next/navigation";
import { ChevronDown, ChevronRight, X } from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import type { NavGroupData, NavItem } from "./nav-config";
import { iconFor } from "./nav-icons";

const PIN_TOP = "/dashboard";
const PIN_BOTTOM = "/analytics";

function NavLink({
  item,
  active,
  onClose,
  label,
  trailing,
}: {
  item: NavItem;
  active: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  label?: string;
  trailing?: boolean;
}) {
  
  return (
    <Link
      href={item.href}
      onClick={onClose}
      className={cn(
        "group flex items-center gap-3 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm transition-all duration-200",
        active
          ? "bg-blue-600 font-medium text-white shadow-lg shadow-blue-950/50"
          : "text-slate-200 hover:translate-x-0.5 hover:bg-white/10 hover:text-white"
      )}
    >
      {createElement(iconFor(item.href), {
        className: cn(
          "h-4.5 w-4.5 shrink-0 motion-safe:group-hover:animate-icon-wiggle",
          active ? "text-white" : "text-sky-300"
        ),
      })}
      <span className="truncate">{label ?? item.label}</span>
      {trailing && (
        <ChevronRight className="ml-auto h-4 w-4 opacity-70 transition-transform duration-200 group-hover:translate-x-1" />
      )}
    </Link>
  );
}

export function Sidebar({
  groups,
  open,
  onClose,
}: {
  groups: NavGroupData[];
  open: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
}) {
  const pathname = usePathname();

  const readyItems = groups.flatMap((g) => g.items.filter((i) => i.ready));
  const top = readyItems.find((i) => i.href === PIN_TOP);
  const bottom = readyItems.find((i) => i.href === PIN_BOTTOM);

  // Dashboard ar Analytics group theke bad (alada pin kora), "soon" item-o bad
  const visible = groups
    .map((g) => ({
      ...g,
      items: g.items.filter((i) => i.ready && i.href !== PIN_TOP && i.href !== PIN_BOTTOM),
    }))
    .filter((g) => g.items.length > 0);

  // shobcheye lomba match-kora href-i active (orders vs orders/amendments)
  const activeHref = readyItems
    .map((i) => i.href)
    .filter((h) => pathname === h || pathname.startsWith(h + "/"))
    .sort((a, b) => b.length - a.length)[0];

  const activeGroup = visible.find((g) => g.items.some((i) => i.href === activeHref))?.label;

  const [openGroups, setOpenGroups] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set(visible.map((g) => g.label)));

  // page change hole oi page-er group auto-open
  const [prevActive, setPrevActive] = useState(activeGroup);
  if (activeGroup !== prevActive) {
    setPrevActive(activeGroup);
    if (activeGroup) setOpenGroups((p) => new Set(p).add(activeGroup));
  }

  function toggle(label: string) {
    setOpenGroups((prev) => {
      const next = new Set(prev);
      if (next.has(label)) next.delete(label);
      else next.add(label);
      return next;
    });
  }

  return (
    <>
      {/* à¦®à§‹à¦¬à¦¾à¦‡à¦²à§‡ à¦®à§‡à¦¨à§ à¦–à§à¦²à¦²à§‡ à¦ªà§‡à¦›à¦¨à§‡à¦° à¦à¦¾à¦ªà¦¸à¦¾ à¦…à¦‚à¦¶ */}
      {open && <div className="fixed inset-0 z-30 bg-black/40 md:hidden" onClick={onClose} />}

      <aside
        className={cn(
          "fixed inset-y-0 left-0 z-40 flex w-64 shrink-0 flex-col bg-linear-to-b from-navy-800 to-navy-950 text-slate-200 transition-transform md:sticky md:top-0 md:h-screen md:translate-x-0",
          open ? "translate-x-0" : "-translate-x-full"
        )}
      >
        {/* Logo */}
        <div className="flex h-16 shrink-0 items-center justify-between px-4">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
            <span className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-linear-to-br from-sky-400 to-blue-600 font-heading text-lg font-bold text-white shadow-lg shadow-blue-950/50">
              M
            </span>
            <div className="leading-tight">
              <p className="font-heading text-base font-bold tracking-wide text-white">
                MERCH<span className="text-sky-300">FLOW</span>
              </p>
              <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-300/80">Garments &amp; Textile ERP</p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <button className="md:hidden" onClick={onClose} aria-label="Close menu">
            <X className="h-5 w-5" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Dashboard: alada pin */}
        {top && (
          <div className="shrink-0 px-3 pb-2 pt-1">
            <NavLink item={top} active={top.href === activeHref} onClose={onClose} />
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Groups */}
        <nav className="flex-1 space-y-1 overflow-y-auto px-3 pb-4 pt-1">
          {visible.map((g) => {
            const isOpen = openGroups.has(g.label);
            return (
              <div key={g.label}>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => toggle(g.label)}
                  aria-expanded={isOpen}
                  className="flex w-full items-center justify-between rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-sky-300/80 transition-colors hover:text-white"
                >
                  {g.label}
                  <ChevronDown className={cn("h-3.5 w-3.5 transition-transform", isOpen && "rotate-180")} />
                </button>
                
                <div
                  className={cn(
                    "grid transition-[grid-template-rows] duration-300 ease-out",
                    isOpen ? "grid-rows-[1fr]" : "grid-rows-[0fr]"
                  )}
                >
                  <ul className="min-h-0 space-y-0.5 overflow-hidden" inert={!isOpen}>
                    {g.items.map((item) => (
                      <li key={item.href}>
                        <NavLink item={item} active={item.href === activeHref} onClose={onClose} />
                      </li>
                    ))}
                    <li aria-hidden="true" className="h-2" />
                  </ul>
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </nav>

        {/* Reports & Analytics: niche pin */}
        {bottom && (
          <div className="shrink-0 border-t border-white/10 p-3">
            <NavLink
              item={bottom}
              active={bottom.href === activeHref}
              onClose={onClose}
              label="Reports & Analytics"
              trailing
            />
          </div>
        )}
      </aside>
    </>
  );
}

===== .\src\components\layout\topbar.tsx =====
"use client";

import { Building2, LogOut, Menu } from "lucide-react";
import { logoutAction, switchCompanyAction } from "@/app/(app)/actions";
import { SearchBox } from "./search-box";
import { NotificationBell, type BellItem } from "./notification-bell";
import { UserMenu } from "./user-menu";

export function Topbar({
  onMenu,
  companyName,
  companies,
  activeCompanyId,
  userEmail,
  userName,
  userRole,
  avatarUrl,
  unreadCount,
  notifications,
}: {
  onMenu: () => void;
  companyName: string;
  companies: { id: string; name: string }[];
  activeCompanyId: string;
  userEmail: string;
  userName: string;
  userRole: string;
  avatarUrl: string | null;
  unreadCount: number;
  notifications: BellItem[];
}) {
  return (
    <header className="sticky top-0 z-20 flex h-14 items-center gap-3 border-b bg-card px-4">
      <button onClick={onMenu} className="md:hidden" aria-label="Open menu">
        <Menu className="h-5 w-5" />
      </button>

      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm">
        <Building2 className="h-4 w-4 text-muted-foreground" />
        {companies.length > 1 ? (
          <form action={switchCompanyAction}>
            <select
              name="companyId"
              defaultValue={activeCompanyId}
              onChange={(e) => e.currentTarget.form?.requestSubmit()}
              className="rounded-md border bg-card px-2 py-1 text-sm"
            >
              {companies.map((c) => (
                <option key={c.id} value={c.id}>
                  {c.name}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </form>
        ) : (
          <span className="font-medium">{companyName}</span>
        )}
      </div>

      <SearchBox />

      <div className="ml-auto flex items-center gap-3">
        <NotificationBell
          key={`${unreadCount}:${notifications.map((n) => `${n.id}${n.read ? 1 : 0}`).join(",")}`}
          unreadCount={unreadCount}
          items={notifications}
        />
        <UserMenu name={userName} email={userEmail} role={userRole} avatarUrl={avatarUrl} />
        <form action={logoutAction}>
          <button className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md border px-2.5 py-1.5 text-sm hover:bg-muted">
            <LogOut className="h-4 w-4" />
            Logout
          </button>
        </form>
      </div>
    </header>
  );
}

===== .\src\components\layout\user-menu.tsx =====
"use client";

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { ChevronDown, ShieldCheck, UserCog } from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import { Avatar } from "./avatar";

export function UserMenu({
  name,
  email,
  role,
  avatarUrl,
}: {
  name: string;
  email: string;
  role: string;
  avatarUrl: string | null;
}) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const onDown = (e: MouseEvent) => {
      if (!ref.current?.contains(e.target as Node)) setOpen(false);
    };
    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === "Escape") setOpen(false);
    };
    document.addEventListener("mousedown", onDown);
    document.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener("mousedown", onDown);
      document.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    };
  }, [open]);

  return (
    <div ref={ref} className="relative">
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={() => setOpen((o) => !o)}
        aria-haspopup="menu"
        aria-expanded={open}
        className="flex items-center gap-2.5 rounded-full py-1 pl-1 pr-2.5 transition-colors hover:bg-muted"
      >
        <Avatar name={name} src={avatarUrl} />
        <span className="hidden text-left leading-tight sm:block">
          <span className="block text-sm font-semibold text-navy-800">{name}</span>
          {role && <span className="block text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">{role}</span>}
        </span>
        <ChevronDown className={cn("h-4 w-4 text-slate-500 transition-transform", open && "rotate-180")} />
      </button>

      {open && (
        <div
          role="menu"
          className="absolute right-0 top-full z-30 mt-2 w-64 rounded-xl border bg-card p-1.5 shadow-xl"
        >
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 border-b px-3 pb-3 pt-2">
            <Avatar name={name} src={avatarUrl} className="h-11 w-11" />
            <div className="min-w-0">
              <p className="truncate text-sm font-semibold text-navy-800">{name}</p>
              <p className="truncate text-xs text-muted-foreground">{email}</p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <Link
            href="/account/profile"
            role="menuitem"
            onClick={() => setOpen(false)}
            className="mt-1 flex items-center gap-2.5 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm hover:bg-muted"
          >
            <UserCog className="h-4 w-4 text-slate-500" /> Edit profile
          </Link>
          <Link
            href="/account/security"
            role="menuitem"
            onClick={() => setOpen(false)}
            className="flex items-center gap-2.5 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm hover:bg-muted"
          >
            <ShieldCheck className="h-4 w-4 text-slate-500" /> Security
          </Link>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

===== .\src\components\ui\badge.tsx =====
import * as React from "react";
import { cva, type VariantProps } from "class-variance-authority";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

export const badgeVariants = cva(
  "inline-flex items-center rounded-full px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium whitespace-nowrap",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        default: "bg-primary text-primary-foreground",
        muted: "bg-muted text-muted-foreground",
        outline: "border text-foreground",
        success: "bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800",
        warning: "bg-gold-100 text-gold-600",
        danger: "bg-red-100 text-red-800",
        info: "bg-denim-100 text-denim-800",
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: { variant: "default" },
  }
);

export type BadgeProps = React.ComponentProps<"span"> & VariantProps<typeof badgeVariants>;

export function Badge({ className, variant, ...props }: BadgeProps) {
  return <span className={cn(badgeVariants({ variant }), className)} {...props} />;
}

===== .\src\components\ui\button.tsx =====
import * as React from "react";
import { cva, type VariantProps } from "class-variance-authority";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

export const buttonVariants = cva(
  "inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 whitespace-nowrap rounded-md text-sm font-medium transition-colors focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring focus-visible:ring-offset-1 disabled:pointer-events-none disabled:opacity-50",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        default: "bg-primary text-primary-foreground hover:bg-denim-700",
        secondary: "bg-secondary text-secondary-foreground hover:bg-denim-100",
        outline: "border border-input bg-card hover:bg-muted",
        ghost: "hover:bg-muted",
        destructive: "bg-destructive text-white hover:opacity-90",
        gold: "bg-gold-500 text-ink hover:bg-gold-400",
      },
      size: {
        default: "h-9 px-4",
        sm: "h-8 px-3 text-xs",
        lg: "h-11 px-6",
        icon: "h-9 w-9",
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: { variant: "default", size: "default" },
  }
);

export type ButtonProps = React.ComponentProps<"button"> & VariantProps<typeof buttonVariants>;

// à¦²à¦¿à¦‚à¦•à¦•à§‡ à¦¬à¦¾à¦Ÿà¦¨à§‡à¦° à¦®à¦¤à§‹ à¦¦à§‡à¦–à¦¾à¦¤à§‡: <Link className={buttonVariants({ variant: "outline" })}>
export function Button({ className, variant, size, ...props }: ButtonProps) {
  return <button className={cn(buttonVariants({ variant, size }), className)} {...props} />;
}

import * as React from "react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

// à¦¸à¦¾à¦§à¦¾à¦°à¦£ à¦¬à§à¦°à¦¾à¦‰à¦œà¦¾à¦°à§‡à¦° select (à¦®à§‹à¦¬à¦¾à¦‡à¦²à§‡ à¦­à¦¾à¦²à§‹ à¦šà¦²à§‡)

===== .\src\components\ui\table.tsx =====
import * as React from "react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

export function Table({ className, ...props }: React.ComponentProps<"table">) {
  return (
    <div className="w-full overflow-x-auto">
      <table className={cn("w-full caption-bottom text-sm", className)} {...props} />
    </div>
  );
}

export function TableHeader({ className, ...props }: React.ComponentProps<"thead">) {
  return <thead className={cn("bg-muted/60 [&_tr]:border-b", className)} {...props} />;
}

export function TableBody({ className, ...props }: React.ComponentProps<"tbody">) {
  return <tbody className={cn("[&_tr:last-child]:border-0", className)} {...props} />;
}

export function TableRow({ className, ...props }: React.ComponentProps<"tr">) {
  return <tr className={cn("border-b transition-colors hover:bg-muted/40", className)} {...props} />;
}

export function TableHead({ className, ...props }: React.ComponentProps<"th">) {
  return (
    <th

    />
  );
}

export function TableCell({ className, ...props }: React.ComponentProps<"td">) {
  return <td className={cn("px-3 py-2.5 align-middle", className)} {...props} />;
}

   * Group by ApprovalAction.

   *     createdAt: true
   *   },
   *   _count: {
   *     _all: true
   *   },
   * })
   * 
  **/
  groupBy<
    T extends ApprovalActionGroupByArgs,
    HasSelectOrTake extends Prisma.Or<
      Prisma.Extends<'skip', Prisma.Keys<T>>,
      Prisma.Extends<'take', Prisma.Keys<T>>
    >,
    OrderByArg extends Prisma.True extends HasSelectOrTake
      ? { orderBy: ApprovalActionGroupByArgs['orderBy'] }
      : { orderBy?: ApprovalActionGroupByArgs['orderBy'] },
    OrderFields extends Prisma.ExcludeUnderscoreKeys<Prisma.Keys<Prisma.MaybeTupleToUnion<T['orderBy']>>>,
    ByFields extends Prisma.MaybeTupleToUnion<T['by']>,
    ByValid extends Prisma.Has<ByFields, OrderFields>,
    HavingFields extends Prisma.GetHavingFields<T['having']>,
    HavingValid extends Prisma.Has<ByFields, HavingFields>,
    ByEmpty extends T['by'] extends never[] ? Prisma.True : Prisma.False,
    InputErrors extends ByEmpty extends Prisma.True
    ? `Error: "by" must not be empty.`
    : HavingValid extends Prisma.False
    ? {
        [P in HavingFields]: P extends ByFields
          ? never
          : P extends string
          ? `Error: Field "${P}" used in "having" needs to be provided in "by".`
          : [
              Error,
              'Field ',
              P,
              ` in "having" needs to be provided in "by"`,
            ]
      }[HavingFields]
    : 'take' extends Prisma.Keys<T>
    ? 'orderBy' extends Prisma.Keys<T>
      ? ByValid extends Prisma.True
        ? {}
        : {
            [P in OrderFields]: P extends ByFields
              ? never
              : `Error: Field "${P}" in "orderBy" needs to be provided in "by"`
          }[OrderFields]
      : 'Error: If you provide "take", you also need to provide "orderBy"'
    : 'skip' extends Prisma.Keys<T>
    ? 'orderBy' extends Prisma.Keys<T>
      ? ByValid extends Prisma.True
        ? {}
        : {
            [P in OrderFields]: P extends ByFields
              ? never
              : `Error: Field "${P}" in "orderBy" needs to be provided in "by"`
          }[OrderFields]
      : 'Error: If you provide "skip", you also need to provide "orderBy"'
    : ByValid extends Prisma.True
    ? {}
    : {
        [P in OrderFields]: P extends ByFields
          ? never
          : `Error: Field "${P}" in "orderBy" needs to be provided in "by"`
      }[OrderFields]
  >(args: Prisma.SubsetIntersection<T, ApprovalActionGroupByArgs, OrderByArg> & InputErrors): {} extends InputErrors ? GetApprovalActionGroupByPayload<T> : Prisma.PrismaPromise<InputErrors>
/**
 * Fields of the ApprovalAction model
 */
readonly fields: ApprovalActionFieldRefs;

/**

 * Why is this prefixed with `Prisma__`?

   * @param onrejected The callback to execute when the Promise is rejected.

export type NotificationAggregateArgs<ExtArgs extends runtime.Types.Extensions.InternalArgs = runtime.Types.Extensions.DefaultArgs> = {
  /**
   * Filter which Notification to aggregate.
   */
  where?: Prisma.NotificationWhereInput
  /**
   * {@link https://www.prisma.io/docs/concepts/components/prisma-client/sorting Sorting Docs}
   * 
   * Determine the order of Notifications to fetch.
   */
  orderBy?: Prisma.NotificationOrderByWithRelationInput | Prisma.NotificationOrderByWithRelationInput[]
  /**
   * {@link https://www.prisma.io/docs/concepts/components/prisma-client/pagination#cursor-based-pagination Cursor Docs}
   * 
   * Sets the start position
   */
  cursor?: Prisma.NotificationWhereUniqueInput
  /**
   * {@link https://www.prisma.io/docs/concepts/components/prisma-client/pagination Pagination Docs}
   * 
   * Take `Â±n` Notifications from the position of the cursor.
   */
  take?: number
  /**
   * {@link https://www.prisma.io/docs/concepts/components/prisma-client/pagination Pagination Docs}
   * 
   * Skip the first `n` Notifications.
   */
  skip?: number
  /**
   * {@link https://www.prisma.io/docs/concepts/components/prisma-client/aggregations Aggregation Docs}
   * 
   * Count returned Notifications
  **/
  _count?: true | NotificationCountAggregateInputType
  /**
   * {@link https://www.prisma.io/docs/concepts/components/prisma-client/aggregations Aggregation Docs}
   * 
   * Select which fields to find the minimum value
  **/
  _min?: NotificationMinAggregateInputType
  /**
   * {@link https://www.prisma.io/docs/concepts/components/prisma-client/aggregations Aggregation Docs}
   * 
   * Select which fields to find the maximum value
  **/
  _max?: NotificationMaxAggregateInputType
}

export type GetNotificationAggregateType<T extends NotificationAggregateArgs> = {
      [P in keyof T & keyof AggregateNotification]: P extends '_count' | 'count'
    ? T[P] extends true
      ? number
      : Prisma.GetScalarType<T[P], AggregateNotification[P]>
    : Prisma.GetScalarType<T[P], AggregateNotification[P]>
}




export type NotificationGroupByArgs<ExtArgs extends runtime.Types.Extensions.InternalArgs = runtime.Types.Extensions.DefaultArgs> = {
  where?: Prisma.NotificationWhereInput
  orderBy?: Prisma.NotificationOrderByWithAggregationInput | Prisma.NotificationOrderByWithAggregationInput[]
  by: Prisma.NotificationScalarFieldEnum[] | Prisma.NotificationScalarFieldEnum
  having?: Prisma.NotificationScalarWhereWithAggregatesInput
  take?: number
  skip?: number
  _count?: NotificationCountAggregateInputType | true
  _min?: NotificationMinAggregateInputType
  _max?: NotificationMaxAggregateInputType
}

  Array<
    Prisma.PickEnumerable<NotificationGroupByOutputType, T['by']> &
      {
        [P in ((keyof T) & (keyof NotificationGroupByOutputType))]: P extends '_count'
          ? T[P] extends boolean
            ? number
            : Prisma.GetScalarType<T[P], NotificationGroupByOutputType[P]>

      }
    >
  >

   *   where: {
   *     // ... the filter for the Notifications we want to count
   *   }
   * })
  **/

    args?: Prisma.Subset<T, NotificationCountArgs>,
  ): Prisma.PrismaPromise<
    T extends runtime.Types.Utils.Record<'select', any>
      ? T['select'] extends true
        ? number
        : Prisma.GetScalarType<T['select'], NotificationCountAggregateOutputType>
      : number
  >

  /**
   * Allows you to perform aggregations operations on a Notification.
   * Note, that providing `undefined` is treated as the value not being there.
   * Read more here: https://pris.ly/d/null-undefined
   * @param {NotificationAggregateArgs} args - Select which aggregations you would like to apply and on what fields.
   * @example
   * // Ordered by age ascending
   * // Where email contains prisma.io
   * // Limited to the 10 users
   * const aggregations = await prisma.user.aggregate({
   *   _avg: {
   *     age: true,
   *   },
   *   where: {
   *     email: {
   *       contains: "prisma.io",
   *     },
   *   },
   *   orderBy: {
   *     age: "asc",
   *   },
   *   take: 10,
   * })
  **/
  aggregate<T extends NotificationAggregateArgs>(args: Prisma.Subset<T, NotificationAggregateArgs>): Prisma.PrismaPromise<GetNotificationAggregateType<T>>

  /**
   * Group by Notification.
   * Note, that providing `undefined` is treated as the value not being there.
   * Read more here: https://pris.ly/d/null-undefined
   * @param {NotificationGroupByArgs} args - Group by arguments.
   * @example
   * // Group by city, order by createdAt, get count
   * const result = await prisma.user.groupBy({
   *   by: ['city', 'createdAt'],
   *   orderBy: {
   *     createdAt: true
   *   },
   *   _count: {
   *     _all: true
   *   },
   * })
   * 
  **/
  groupBy<
    T extends NotificationGroupByArgs,
    HasSelectOrTake extends Prisma.Or<
      Prisma.Extends<'skip', Prisma.Keys<T>>,
      Prisma.Extends<'take', Prisma.Keys<T>>
    >,
    OrderByArg extends Prisma.True extends HasSelectOrTake
      ? { orderBy: NotificationGroupByArgs['orderBy'] }
      : { orderBy?: NotificationGroupByArgs['orderBy'] },
    OrderFields extends Prisma.ExcludeUnderscoreKeys<Prisma.Keys<Prisma.MaybeTupleToUnion<T['orderBy']>>>,
    ByFields extends Prisma.MaybeTupleToUnion<T['by']>,
    ByValid extends Prisma.Has<ByFields, OrderFields>,
    HavingFields extends Prisma.GetHavingFields<T['having']>,
    HavingValid extends Prisma.Has<ByFields, HavingFields>,
    ByEmpty extends T['by'] extends never[] ? Prisma.True : Prisma.False,
    InputErrors extends ByEmpty extends Prisma.True
    ? `Error: "by" must not be empty.`
    : HavingValid extends Prisma.False
    ? {
        [P in HavingFields]: P extends ByFields
          ? never
          : P extends string
          ? `Error: Field "${P}" used in "having" needs to be provided in "by".`
          : [
              Error,
              'Field ',
              P,
              ` in "having" needs to be provided in "by"`,
            ]
      }[HavingFields]
    : 'take' extends Prisma.Keys<T>
    ? 'orderBy' extends Prisma.Keys<T>
      ? ByValid extends Prisma.True
        ? {}
        : {
            [P in OrderFields]: P extends ByFields
              ? never
              : `Error: Field "${P}" in "orderBy" needs to be provided in "by"`
          }[OrderFields]
      : 'Error: If you provide "take", you also need to provide "orderBy"'
    : 'skip' extends Prisma.Keys<T>
    ? 'orderBy' extends Prisma.Keys<T>
      ? ByValid extends Prisma.True
        ? {}
        : {
            [P in OrderFields]: P extends ByFields
              ? never
              : `Error: Field "${P}" in "orderBy" needs to be provided in "by"`
          }[OrderFields]
      : 'Error: If you provide "skip", you also need to provide "orderBy"'
    : ByValid extends Prisma.True
    ? {}
    : {
        [P in OrderFields]: P extends ByFields
          ? never
          : `Error: Field "${P}" in "orderBy" needs to be provided in "by"`
      }[OrderFields]
  >(args: Prisma.SubsetIntersection<T, NotificationGroupByArgs, OrderByArg> & InputErrors): {} extends InputErrors ? GetNotificationGroupByPayload<T> : Prisma.PrismaPromise<InputErrors>
/**
 * Fields of the Notification model
 */
readonly fields: NotificationFieldRefs;
}

   * const aggregations = await prisma.user.aggregate({
   *   _avg: {

   *   },

   *     email: {
   *       contains: "prisma.io",
   *     },
   *   },
   *   orderBy: {

   *   },
   *   take: 10,

  /**
   * Group by NotificationPreference.
   * Note, that providing `undefined` is treated as the value not being there.
   * Read more here: https://pris.ly/d/null-undefined
   * @param {NotificationPreferenceGroupByArgs} args - Group by arguments.
   * @example
   * // Group by city, order by createdAt, get count
   * const result = await prisma.user.groupBy({
   *   by: ['city', 'createdAt'],
   *   orderBy: {
   *     createdAt: true
   *   },
   *   _count: {
   *     _all: true
   *   },
   * })
   * 
  **/
  groupBy<
    T extends NotificationPreferenceGroupByArgs,
    HasSelectOrTake extends Prisma.Or<
      Prisma.Extends<'skip', Prisma.Keys<T>>,
      Prisma.Extends<'take', Prisma.Keys<T>>
    >,
    OrderByArg extends Prisma.True extends HasSelectOrTake
      ? { orderBy: NotificationPreferenceGroupByArgs['orderBy'] }
      : { orderBy?: NotificationPreferenceGroupByArgs['orderBy'] },
    OrderFields extends Prisma.ExcludeUnderscoreKeys<Prisma.Keys<Prisma.MaybeTupleToUnion<T['orderBy']>>>,
    ByFields extends Prisma.MaybeTupleToUnion<T['by']>,
    ByValid extends Prisma.Has<ByFields, OrderFields>,
    HavingFields extends Prisma.GetHavingFields<T['having']>,
    HavingValid extends Prisma.Has<ByFields, HavingFields>,
    ByEmpty extends T['by'] extends never[] ? Prisma.True : Prisma.False,
    InputErrors extends ByEmpty extends Prisma.True
    ? `Error: "by" must not be empty.`
    : HavingValid extends Prisma.False
    ? {
        [P in HavingFields]: P extends ByFields
          ? never
          : P extends string
          ? `Error: Field "${P}" used in "having" needs to be provided in "by".`
          : [
              Error,
              'Field ',
              P,
              ` in "having" needs to be provided in "by"`,
            ]
      }[HavingFields]
    : 'take' extends Prisma.Keys<T>
    ? 'orderBy' extends Prisma.Keys<T>
      ? ByValid extends Prisma.True
        ? {}
        : {
            [P in OrderFields]: P extends ByFields
              ? never
              : `Error: Field "${P}" in "orderBy" needs to be provided in "by"`
          }[OrderFields]
      : 'Error: If you provide "take", you also need to provide "orderBy"'
    : 'skip' extends Prisma.Keys<T>
    ? 'orderBy' extends Prisma.Keys<T>
      ? ByValid extends Prisma.True
        ? {}
        : {
            [P in OrderFields]: P extends ByFields
              ? never
              : `Error: Field "${P}" in "orderBy" needs to be provided in "by"`
          }[OrderFields]
      : 'Error: If you provide "skip", you also need to provide "orderBy"'
    : ByValid extends Prisma.True
    ? {}
    : {
        [P in OrderFields]: P extends ByFields
          ? never
          : `Error: Field "${P}" in "orderBy" needs to be provided in "by"`
      }[OrderFields]
  >(args: Prisma.SubsetIntersection<T, NotificationPreferenceGroupByArgs, OrderByArg> & InputErrors): {} extends InputErrors ? GetNotificationPreferenceGroupByPayload<T> : Prisma.PrismaPromise<InputErrors>

  upsert<T extends SampleApprovalUpsertArgs>(args: Prisma.SelectSubset<T, SampleApprovalUpsertArgs<ExtArgs>>): Prisma.Prisma__SampleApprovalClient<runtime.Types.Result.GetResult<Prisma.$SampleApprovalPayload<ExtArgs>, T, "upsert", GlobalOmitOptions>, never, ExtArgs, GlobalOmitOptions>


  /**
   * Count the number of SampleApprovals.
   * Note, that providing `undefined` is treated as the value not being there.
   * Read more here: https://pris.ly/d/null-undefined
   * @param {SampleApprovalCountArgs} args - Arguments to filter SampleApprovals to count.
   * @example
   * // Count the number of SampleApprovals
   * const count = await prisma.sampleApproval.count({
   *   where: {
   *     // ... the filter for the SampleApprovals we want to count
   *   }
   * })
  **/
  count<T extends SampleApprovalCountArgs>(
    args?: Prisma.Subset<T, SampleApprovalCountArgs>,
  ): Prisma.PrismaPromise<
    T extends runtime.Types.Utils.Record<'select', any>
      ? T['select'] extends true
        ? number
        : Prisma.GetScalarType<T['select'], SampleApprovalCountAggregateOutputType>
      : number
  >

  /**
   * Allows you to perform aggregations operations on a SampleApproval.
   * Note, that providing `undefined` is treated as the value not being there.
   * Read more here: https://pris.ly/d/null-undefined
   * @param {SampleApprovalAggregateArgs} args - Select which aggregations you would like to apply and on what fields.
   * @example
   * // Ordered by age ascending
   * // Where email contains prisma.io
   * // Limited to the 10 users
   * const aggregations = await prisma.user.aggregate({
   *   _avg: {
   *     age: true,
   *   },
   *   where: {
   *     email: {
   *       contains: "prisma.io",
   *     },
   *   },
   *   orderBy: {
   *     age: "asc",
   *   },
   *   take: 10,
   * })
  **/
  aggregate<T extends SampleApprovalAggregateArgs>(args: Prisma.Subset<T, SampleApprovalAggregateArgs>): Prisma.PrismaPromise<GetSampleApprovalAggregateType<T>>

  /**
   * Group by SampleApproval.
   * Note, that providing `undefined` is treated as the value not being there.
   * Read more here: https://pris.ly/d/null-undefined
   * @param {SampleApprovalGroupByArgs} args - Group by arguments.
   * @example
   * // Group by city, order by createdAt, get count
   * const result = await prisma.user.groupBy({
   *   by: ['city', 'createdAt'],
   *   orderBy: {
   *     createdAt: true
   *   },
   *   _count: {
   *     _all: true
   *   },
   * })
   * 
  **/
  groupBy<
    T extends SampleApprovalGroupByArgs,
    HasSelectOrTake extends Prisma.Or<
      Prisma.Extends<'skip', Prisma.Keys<T>>,
      Prisma.Extends<'take', Prisma.Keys<T>>
    >,
    OrderByArg extends Prisma.True extends HasSelectOrTake
      ? { orderBy: SampleApprovalGroupByArgs['orderBy'] }
      : { orderBy?: SampleApprovalGroupByArgs['orderBy'] },
    OrderFields extends Prisma.ExcludeUnderscoreKeys<Prisma.Keys<Prisma.MaybeTupleToUnion<T['orderBy']>>>,
    ByFields extends Prisma.MaybeTupleToUnion<T['by']>,
    ByValid extends Prisma.Has<ByFields, OrderFields>,
    HavingFields extends Prisma.GetHavingFields<T['having']>,
    HavingValid extends Prisma.Has<ByFields, HavingFields>,
    ByEmpty extends T['by'] extends never[] ? Prisma.True : Prisma.False,
    InputErrors extends ByEmpty extends Prisma.True
    ? `Error: "by" must not be empty.`
    : HavingValid extends Prisma.False
    ? {
        [P in HavingFields]: P extends ByFields
          ? never
          : P extends string
          ? `Error: Field "${P}" used in "having" needs to be provided in "by".`
          : [
              Error,
              'Field ',
              P,
              ` in "having" needs to be provided in "by"`,
            ]
      }[HavingFields]
    : 'take' extends Prisma.Keys<T>
    ? 'orderBy' extends Prisma.Keys<T>
      ? ByValid extends Prisma.True
        ? {}
        : {
            [P in OrderFields]: P extends ByFields
              ? never
              : `Error: Field "${P}" in "orderBy" needs to be provided in "by"`
          }[OrderFields]
      : 'Error: If you provide "take", you also need to provide "orderBy"'
    : 'skip' extends Prisma.Keys<T>
    ? 'orderBy' extends Prisma.Keys<T>
      ? ByValid extends Prisma.True
        ? {}
        : {
            [P in OrderFields]: P extends ByFields
              ? never
              : `Error: Field "${P}" in "orderBy" needs to be provided in "by"`
          }[OrderFields]
      : 'Error: If you provide "skip", you also need to provide "orderBy"'
    : ByValid extends Prisma.True
    ? {}
    : {
        [P in OrderFields]: P extends ByFields
          ? never
          : `Error: Field "${P}" in "orderBy" needs to be provided in "by"`
      }[OrderFields]
  >(args: Prisma.SubsetIntersection<T, SampleApprovalGroupByArgs, OrderByArg> & InputErrors): {} extends InputErrors ? GetSampleApprovalGroupByPayload<T> : Prisma.PrismaPromise<InputErrors>
/**
 * Fields of the SampleApproval model
 */
readonly fields: SampleApprovalFieldRefs;
}

/**
 * The delegate class that acts as a "Promise-like" for SampleApproval.
 * Why is this prefixed with `Prisma__`?
 * Because we want to prevent naming conflicts as mentioned in
 * https://github.com/prisma/prisma-client-js/issues/707
 */
export interface Prisma__SampleApprovalClient<T, Null = never, ExtArgs extends runtime.Types.Extensions.InternalArgs = runtime.Types.Extensions.DefaultArgs, GlobalOmitOptions = {}> extends Prisma.PrismaPromise<T> {
  readonly [Symbol.toStringTag]: "PrismaPromise"
  sample<T extends Prisma.SampleRequestDefaultArgs<ExtArgs> = {}>(args?: Prisma.Subset<T, Prisma.SampleRequestDefaultArgs<ExtArgs>>): Prisma.Prisma__SampleRequestClient<runtime.Types.Result.GetResult<Prisma.$SampleRequestPayload<ExtArgs>, T, "findUniqueOrThrow", GlobalOmitOptions> | Null, Null, ExtArgs, GlobalOmitOptions>
  /**
   * Attaches callbacks for the resolution and/or rejection of the Promise.
   * @param onfulfilled The callback to execute when the Promise is resolved.
   * @param onrejected The callback to execute when the Promise is rejected.
   * @returns A Promise for the completion of which ever callback is executed.
   */
  then<TResult1 = T, TResult2 = never>(onfulfilled?: ((value: T) => TResult1 | PromiseLike<TResult1>) | undefined | null, onrejected?: ((reason: any) => TResult2 | PromiseLike<TResult2>) | undefined | null): runtime.Types.Utils.JsPromise<TResult1 | TResult2>
  /**
   * Attaches a callback for only the rejection of the Promise.
   * @param onrejected The callback to execute when the Promise is rejected.
   * @returns A Promise for the completion of the callback.
   */
  catch<TResult = never>(onrejected?: ((reason: any) => TResult | PromiseLike<TResult>) | undefined | null): runtime.Types.Utils.JsPromise<T | TResult>
  /**
   * Attaches a callback that is invoked when the Promise is settled (fulfilled or rejected). The
   * resolved value cannot be modified from the callback.
   * @param onfinally The callback to execute when the Promise is settled (fulfilled or rejected).
   * @returns A Promise for the completion of the callback.
   */
  finally(onfinally?: (() => void) | undefined | null): runtime.Types.Utils.JsPromise<T>
}

===== .\src\lib\approval-calc.test.ts =====
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { daysPending, delegationCovers, shouldEscalate } from "./approval-calc";

const d = (s: string) => new Date(`${s}T00:00:00.000Z`);

describe("delegation date range", () => {
  const del = { startDate: d("2026-11-01"), endDate: d("2026-11-05"), isActive: true };
  it("includes both end dates", () => {
    expect(delegationCovers(del, d("2026-11-01"))).toBe(true);
    expect(delegationCovers(del, d("2026-11-05"))).toBe(true);
  });
  it("excludes days outside the range", () => {
    expect(delegationCovers(del, d("2026-10-31"))).toBe(false);
    expect(delegationCovers(del, d("2026-11-06"))).toBe(false);
  });
  it("revoked delegation never covers", () => {
    expect(delegationCovers({ ...del, isActive: false }, d("2026-11-02"))).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe("escalation", () => {
  it("counts whole days pending", () => {
    expect(daysPending(d("2026-11-01"), d("2026-11-04"))).toBe(3);
  });
  it("fires only when pending is more than N days", () => {
    expect(shouldEscalate(d("2026-11-01"), d("2026-11-03"), 2, false)).toBe(false);
    expect(shouldEscalate(d("2026-11-01"), d("2026-11-04"), 2, false)).toBe(true);
  });
  it("does not fire twice, or when the rule is off", () => {
    expect(shouldEscalate(d("2026-11-01"), d("2026-11-10"), 2, true)).toBe(false);
    expect(shouldEscalate(d("2026-11-01"), d("2026-11-10"), null, false)).toBe(false);
  });
});

===== .\src\lib\approval-calc.ts =====
// Approval delegation / escalation hisab. DB nei, tai test kora jay.
export const utcDay = (d: Date) => new Date(Date.UTC(d.getUTCFullYear(), d.getUTCMonth(), d.getUTCDate()));

// Delegation date range-er moddhe (duto prantoi dhora hoy) ar active
export function delegationCovers(d: { startDate: Date; endDate: Date; isActive: boolean }, today: Date): boolean {
  if (!d.isActive) return false;
  const t = utcDay(today).getTime();
  return t >= utcDay(d.startDate).getTime() && t <= utcDay(d.endDate).getTime();
}

export function daysPending(since: Date, now: Date): number {
  return Math.floor((utcDay(now).getTime() - utcDay(since).getTime()) / 86_400_000);
}

// Pending > N din hole escalate (ekbar-i per step). afterDays null/0 hole off
export function shouldEscalate(since: Date, now: Date, afterDays: number | null | undefined, already: boolean): boolean {
  if (!afterDays || afterDays <= 0 || already) return false;
  return daysPending(since, now) > afterDays;
}

===== .\src\services\approval.service.ts =====
import { z } from "zod";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { can } from "@/lib/permissions";
import { BusinessRuleError } from "@/lib/business-rules";
import { BaseService } from "./base.service";
import { notify } from "./notification.service";
import { daysPending, delegationCovers, shouldEscalate } from "@/lib/approval-calc";

const submitSchema = z.object({
  entityType: z.string().min(1),
  entityId: z.string().min(1),
  title: z.string().min(1).max(200),
  description: z.string().max(1000).optional(),
  amount: z.number().nonnegative().optional(),
});

const workflowSchema = z
  .object({
    code: z.string().min(1).max(30),
    name: z.string().min(1).max(100),
    entityType: z.string().min(1),
    minAmount: z.number().nonnegative().optional(),
    maxAmount: z.number().positive().optional(),
    escalateAfterDays: z.number().int().positive().max(60).optional(),
    steps: z
      .array(z.object({ name: z.string().min(1), approverRoleId: z.string().min(1) }))
      .min(1),
  })
  .refine(
    (w) => w.maxAmount === undefined || w.minAmount === undefined || w.maxAmount > w.minAmount,
    { message: "maxAmount must be greater than minAmount" }
  );

// à¦Ÿà¦¾à¦•à¦¾à¦° à¦…à¦‚à¦• à¦…à¦¨à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦¯à¦¼à§€ à¦®à¦¾à¦¨à¦¾à¦¨à¦¸à¦‡ à¦¨à¦¿à¦¯à¦¼à¦® à¦–à§‹à¦à¦œà§‡ (à¦¨à§à¦¯à§‚à¦¨à¦¤à¦® à¦¸à¦¹, à¦¸à¦°à§à¦¬à§‹à¦šà§à¦š à¦¬à¦¾à¦¦à§‡)
async function findWorkflow(companyId: string, entityType: string, amount?: number) {
  const list = await prisma.workflow.findMany({
    where: { companyId, entityType, isActive: true },
    include: { steps: { orderBy: { stepOrder: "asc" } } },
  });
  const a = amount ?? 0;
  return (
    list.find((w) => {
      const min = w.minAmount === null ? 0 : Number(w.minAmount);
      const max = w.maxAmount === null ? Infinity : Number(w.maxAmount);
      return a >= min && a < max && w.steps.length > 0;
    }) ?? null
  );
}

class ApprovalService extends BaseService {
  private async notifyApprovers(
    companyId: string,
    roleId: string,
    req: { id: string; title: string; entityType: string; entityId: string },
    title = "Approval pending"
  ) {
    const approvers = await prisma.user.findMany({
      where: { companyId, roleId, status: "ACTIVE" },
      select: { id: true },
    });
    for (const a of approvers) {
      await notify({
        companyId,
        recipientId: a.id,
        type: "APPROVAL_PENDING",
        title,
        message: req.title,
        href: `/approvals/${req.id}`,
        entityType: req.entityType,
        entityId: req.entityId,
      });
    }
  }

  // à¦¨à¦¿à¦¯à¦¼à¦® à¦¤à§ˆà¦°à¦¿ (à¦¯à§‡à¦®à¦¨ PO à§«,à§¦à§¦à§¦ à¦¥à§‡à¦•à§‡ à§«à§¦,à§¦à§¦à§¦ à¦¹à¦²à§‡ Manager â†’ Head of Procurement)
  async createWorkflow(input: unknown) {
    const user = await this.guard("approval.manage");
    const data = workflowSchema.parse(input);
    const row = await prisma.workflow.create({
      data: {
        companyId: user.companyId,
        code: data.code,
        name: data.name,
        entityType: data.entityType,
        minAmount: data.minAmount,
        maxAmount: data.maxAmount,
        escalateAfterDays: data.escalateAfterDays,
        steps: {
          create: data.steps.map((s, i) => ({
            stepOrder: i + 1,
            name: s.name,
            approverRoleId: s.approverRoleId,
          })),
        },
      },
      include: { steps: true },
    });
    await this.audit({ user, action: "CREATE", entity: "Workflow", entityId: row.id, after: row });
    return row;
  }

  async listWorkflows() {
    const user = await this.guard("approval.manage");
    return prisma.workflow.findMany({
      where: { companyId: user.companyId },
      include: { steps: { orderBy: { stepOrder: "asc" } } },
      orderBy: { entityType: "asc" },
    });
  }

  // à¦…à¦¨à§à¦®à§‹à¦¦à¦¨à§‡à¦° à¦…à¦¨à§à¦°à§‹à¦§ à¦œà¦®à¦¾
  async submit(input: unknown) {
    const user = await this.guard("approval.request");
    const data = submitSchema.parse(input);

    const pending = await prisma.approvalRequest.findFirst({
      where: {
        companyId: user.companyId,
        entityType: data.entityType,
        entityId: data.entityId,
        status: "PENDING",
      },
    });
    if (pending) throw new BusinessRuleError("An approval is already pending for this record");

    const workflow = await findWorkflow(user.companyId, data.entityType, data.amount);
    if (!workflow) {
      throw new BusinessRuleError("No approval workflow is set for this record type and amount");
    }

    const first = workflow.steps[0];
    const req = await prisma.approvalRequest.create({
      data: {
        companyId: user.companyId,
        workflowId: workflow.id,
        entityType: data.entityType,
        entityId: data.entityId,
        title: data.title,
        description: data.description,
        amount: data.amount,
        currentStep: first.stepOrder,
        requestedById: user.id,
      },
    });
    await this.notifyApprovers(user.companyId, first.approverRoleId, req);
    await this.audit({ user, action: "CREATE", entity: "ApprovalRequest", entityId: req.id, after: req });
    return req;
  }

  // à¦…à¦¨à§à¦®à§‹à¦¦à¦¨ à¦¬à¦¾ à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¤à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦–à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦¨ (à¦ªà§à¦°à¦¤à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦–à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦¨à§‡ à¦•à¦¾à¦°à¦£ à¦²à¦¾à¦—à¦¬à§‡)
  async decide(requestId: string, decision: "APPROVED" | "REJECTED", note?: string) {
    const user = await this.guard("approval.decide");
    if (decision === "REJECTED" && (!note || note.trim().length < 3)) {
      throw new BusinessRuleError("Rejection reason is required");
    }

    const req = await prisma.approvalRequest.findUnique({
      where: { id: requestId },
      include: { workflow: { include: { steps: { orderBy: { stepOrder: "asc" } } } } },
    });
    if (!req || req.companyId !== user.companyId) {
      throw new BusinessRuleError("Approval request not found");
    }
    if (req.status !== "PENDING") throw new BusinessRuleError("This request is already decided");

    const steps = req.workflow?.steps ?? [];
    const step = steps.find((s) => s.stepOrder === req.currentStep);
    if (!step) throw new BusinessRuleError("Approval step not found");
    let delegatedFromId: string | undefined;
    if (step.approverRoleId !== user.roleId && !can(user, "*")) {
      const from = (await this.activeDelegations(user)).find((x) => x.fromUser.roleId === step.approverRoleId);
      if (!from) throw new BusinessRuleError("You are not the approver for this step");
      delegatedFromId = from.fromUserId;
    }
    const next = steps.find((s) => s.stepOrder > step.stepOrder);

    const updated = await prisma.$transaction(async (tx) => {
      await tx.approvalAction.create({
        data: { requestId, stepOrder: step.stepOrder, approverId: user.id, delegatedFromId, decision, note },
      });
      if (decision === "REJECTED") {
        return tx.approvalRequest.update({
          where: { id: requestId },
          data: { status: "REJECTED", decidedAt: new Date() },
        });
      }
      if (!next) {
        return tx.approvalRequest.update({
          where: { id: requestId },
          data: { status: "APPROVED", decidedAt: new Date() },
        });
      }
      return tx.approvalRequest.update({
        where: { id: requestId },
        data: { currentStep: next.stepOrder },
      });
    });

    if (decision === "APPROVED" && next) {
      await this.notifyApprovers(updated.companyId, next.approverRoleId, updated);
    } else {
      await notify({
        companyId: updated.companyId,
        recipientId: updated.requestedById,
        title: `Request ${updated.status.toLowerCase()}`,
        message: updated.title,
        href: `/approvals/${updated.id}`,
        entityType: updated.entityType,
        entityId: updated.entityId,
      });
    }

    await this.audit({
      user,
      action: "UPDATE",
      entity: "ApprovalRequest",
      entityId: requestId,
      before: { status: req.status, currentStep: req.currentStep },
      after: { status: updated.status, currentStep: updated.currentStep, decidedBy: user.id, onBehalfOf: delegatedFromId },
      reason: note,
    });
    return updated;
  }

  // à¦†à¦®à¦¾à¦° à¦…à¦¨à§à¦®à§‹à¦¦à¦¨à§‡à¦° à¦…à¦ªà§‡à¦•à§à¦·à¦¾à¦¯à¦¼ à¦¥à¦¾à¦•à¦¾ à¦…à¦¨à§à¦°à§‹à¦§
  async listPending() {
    const user = await this.guard("approval.decide");
    const list = await prisma.approvalRequest.findMany({
      where: { companyId: user.companyId, status: "PENDING" },
      include: { workflow: { include: { steps: true } } },
      orderBy: { requestedAt: "asc" },
    });
    const roleIds = new Set([user.roleId, ...(await this.activeDelegations(user)).map((x) => x.fromUser.roleId)]);
    return list.filter((r) => {
      const step = r.workflow?.steps.find((s) => s.stepOrder === r.currentStep);
      return !!step && (roleIds.has(step.approverRoleId) || can(user, "*"));
    });
  }

  // à¦†à¦®à¦¿ à¦¯à§‡à¦—à§à¦²à§‹ à¦œà¦®à¦¾ à¦¦à¦¿à¦¯à¦¼à§‡à¦›à¦¿
  async listMine() {
    const user = await this.guard("approval.request");
    return prisma.approvalRequest.findMany({
      where: { companyId: user.companyId, requestedById: user.id },
      orderBy: { requestedAt: "desc" },
    });
  }

    // ---------- Delegation ----------
  private async activeDelegations(user: { id: string; companyId: string }) {
    const list = await prisma.approvalDelegation.findMany({
      where: { companyId: user.companyId, toUserId: user.id, isActive: true },
      include: { fromUser: { select: { id: true, roleId: true, status: true } } },
    });
    const now = new Date();
    return list.filter((d) => d.fromUser.status === "ACTIVE" && delegationCovers(d, now));
  }

  async delegate(input: unknown) {
    const user = await this.guard("approval.decide");
    const d = z
      .object({
        toUserId: z.string().min(1),
        startDate: z.coerce.date(),
        endDate: z.coerce.date(),
        reason: z.string().min(3).max(300),
      })
      .refine((v) => v.endDate >= v.startDate, { message: "End date must be on or after the start date" })
      .parse(input);
    if (d.toUserId === user.id) throw new BusinessRuleError("You cannot delegate to yourself");
    const to = await prisma.user.findUnique({ where: { id: d.toUserId } });
    if (!to || to.companyId !== user.companyId || to.status !== "ACTIVE") {
      throw new BusinessRuleError("Delegate user not found");
    }
    const row = await prisma.approvalDelegation.create({
      data: { companyId: user.companyId, fromUserId: user.id, toUserId: to.id, startDate: d.startDate, endDate: d.endDate, reason: d.reason, createdById: user.id },
    });
    await notify({
      companyId: user.companyId,
      recipientId: to.id,
      title: "Approval delegated to you",
      message: `${user.email} delegated approvals to you until ${d.endDate.toISOString().slice(0, 10)}`,
      href: "/approvals",
    });
    await this.audit({ user, action: "CREATE", entity: "ApprovalDelegation", entityId: row.id, after: row, reason: d.reason });
    return row;
  }

  async revokeDelegation(id: string) {
    const user = await this.guard("approval.decide");
    const row = await prisma.approvalDelegation.findUnique({ where: { id } });
    if (!row || row.companyId !== user.companyId || row.fromUserId !== user.id) {
      throw new BusinessRuleError("Delegation not found");
    }
    const saved = await prisma.approvalDelegation.update({ where: { id }, data: { isActive: false, revokedAt: new Date() } });
    await this.audit({ user, action: "UPDATE", entity: "ApprovalDelegation", entityId: id, before: row, after: saved });
    return saved;
  }

  async listDelegations() {
    const user = await this.guard("approval.decide");
    const [given, received] = await Promise.all([
      prisma.approvalDelegation.findMany({ where: { companyId: user.companyId, fromUserId: user.id }, include: { toUser: { select: { name: true, email: true } } }, orderBy: { startDate: "desc" } }),
      prisma.approvalDelegation.findMany({ where: { companyId: user.companyId, toUserId: user.id }, include: { fromUser: { select: { name: true, email: true } } }, orderBy: { startDate: "desc" } }),
    ]);
    return { given, received };
  }

  // ---------- Escalation: pending > N din -> porer step-er role-ke notify ----------
  // ---------- Escalation: pending > N din -> porer step-er role-ke notify ----------
  async escalateOverdue(now = new Date()) {
    const user = await this.guard("approval.manage");
    const list = await prisma.approvalRequest.findMany({
      where: { companyId: user.companyId, status: "PENDING", workflow: { escalateAfterDays: { not: null } } },
      include: {
        workflow: { include: { steps: { orderBy: { stepOrder: "asc" } } } },
        actions: { orderBy: { decidedAt: "desc" }, take: 1 },
        escalations: true,
      },
    });
    let escalated = 0;
    for (const r of list) {
      const since = r.actions[0]?.decidedAt ?? r.requestedAt;
      const already = r.escalations.some((e) => e.stepOrder === r.currentStep);
      if (!shouldEscalate(since, now, r.workflow?.escalateAfterDays, already)) continue;
      const next = (r.workflow?.steps ?? []).find((s) => s.stepOrder > r.currentStep);
      await prisma.approvalEscalation.create({
        data: { requestId: r.id, stepOrder: r.currentStep, escalatedToRoleId: next?.approverRoleId },
      });
      if (next) {
        await this.notifyApprovers(r.companyId, next.approverRoleId, r, "Approval overdue (escalated)");
      } else {
        await notify({
          companyId: r.companyId,
          recipientId: r.requestedById,
          title: "Approval overdue",
          message: r.title,
          href: `/approvals/${r.id}`,
          entityType: r.entityType,
          entityId: r.entityId,
        });
      }
      await this.audit({
        user, action: "UPDATE", entity: "ApprovalRequest", entityId: r.id,
        after: { escalatedStep: r.currentStep, toRoleId: next?.approverRoleId ?? null },
        reason: `Pending more than ${r.workflow?.escalateAfterDays} day(s)`,
      });
      escalated++;
    }
    return { escalated };
  }

  // ---------- "Who is pending" ----------
  async requestDetail(id: string) {
    let user;
    try {
      user = await this.guard("approval.decide");
    } catch {
      user = await this.guard("approval.request");
    }
    const req = await prisma.approvalRequest.findUnique({
      where: { id },
      include: {
        requestedBy: { select: { name: true, email: true } },
        workflow: { include: { steps: { orderBy: { stepOrder: "asc" }, include: { approverRole: true } } } },
        actions: { orderBy: { decidedAt: "asc" }, include: { approver: { select: { id: true, name: true, email: true } } } },
        escalations: true,
      },
    });
    if (!req || req.companyId !== user.companyId) throw new BusinessRuleError("Approval request not found");
    const steps = req.workflow?.steps ?? [];
    const current = steps.find((s) => s.stepOrder === req.currentStep);
    const waitingOn: { id: string; name: string; email: string; via: string | null }[] = [];
    if (req.status === "PENDING" && current) {
      const holders = await prisma.user.findMany({
        where: { companyId: req.companyId, roleId: current.approverRoleId, status: "ACTIVE" },
        select: { id: true, name: true, email: true },
      });
      const dels = (
        await prisma.approvalDelegation.findMany({
          where: { companyId: req.companyId, isActive: true, fromUserId: { in: holders.map((h) => h.id) } },
          include: { toUser: { select: { id: true, name: true, email: true } } },
        })
      ).filter((x) => delegationCovers(x, new Date()));
      for (const h of holders) waitingOn.push({ ...h, via: null });
      for (const x of dels) {
        waitingOn.push({ ...x.toUser, via: `Delegate of ${holders.find((h) => h.id === x.fromUserId)?.name ?? "approver"}` });
      }
    }
    const since = req.actions.length ? req.actions[req.actions.length - 1].decidedAt : req.requestedAt;
    return { req, steps, current, waitingOn, pendingDays: req.status === "PENDING" ? daysPending(since, new Date()) : 0 };
  }
  
    // Workflow-e escalation din set/off (null = off)
  async setEscalation(workflowId: string, days: number | null) {
    const user = await this.guard("approval.manage");
    const v = z.number().int().positive().max(60).nullable().parse(days);
    const before = await prisma.workflow.findUnique({ where: { id: workflowId } });
    if (!before || before.companyId !== user.companyId) throw new BusinessRuleError("Workflow not found");
    const saved = await prisma.workflow.update({ where: { id: workflowId }, data: { escalateAfterDays: v } });
    await this.audit({ user, action: "UPDATE", entity: "Workflow", entityId: workflowId, before: { escalateAfterDays: before.escalateAfterDays }, after: { escalateAfterDays: saved.escalateAfterDays } });
    return saved;
  }
  
  async setWorkflowActive(workflowId: string, active: boolean) {
    const user = await this.guard("approval.manage");
    const before = await prisma.workflow.findUnique({ where: { id: workflowId } });
    if (!before || before.companyId !== user.companyId) throw new BusinessRuleError("Workflow not found");
    const saved = await prisma.workflow.update({ where: { id: workflowId }, data: { isActive: active } });
    await this.audit({
      user, action: "UPDATE", entity: "Workflow", entityId: workflowId,
      before: { isActive: before.isActive }, after: { isActive: saved.isActive },
    });
    return saved;
  }
}

export const approvalService = new ApprovalService();

===== .\src\services\notification.service.ts =====
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { requireUser } from "@/lib/permissions";
import type { NotificationType } from "@/generated/prisma/enums";

type NotifyInput = {
  companyId: string;
  recipientId: string;
  type?: NotificationType;
  title: string;
  message: string;
  href?: string;
  entityType?: string;
  entityId?: string;
};

// à¦…à¦¨à§à¦¯ à¦¸à¦¾à¦°à§à¦­à¦¿à¦¸ à¦¥à§‡à¦•à§‡ à¦¡à¦¾à¦•à¦¾à¦° à¦œà¦¨à§à¦¯à¥¤ à¦¬à§à¦¯à¦¬à¦¹à¦¾à¦°à¦•à¦¾à¦°à§€à¦° à¦ªà¦›à¦¨à§à¦¦ à¦®à§‡à¦¨à§‡ In-app à¦†à¦° Email à¦¸à¦¾à¦°à¦¿ à¦¬à¦¾à¦¨à¦¾à¦¯à¦¼à¥¤
export async function notify(input: NotifyInput) {
  const type = input.type ?? "GENERAL";
  const pref = await prisma.notificationPreference.findUnique({
    where: { userId_type: { userId: input.recipientId, type } },
  });
  const wantInApp = pref?.inApp ?? true;
  const wantEmail = pref?.email ?? false;

  const base = {
    companyId: input.companyId,
    recipientId: input.recipientId,
    type,
    title: input.title,
    message: input.message,
    href: input.href,
    entityType: input.entityType,
    entityId: input.entityId,
  };

  const rows = [];
  if (wantInApp) rows.push({ ...base, channel: "IN_APP" as const, sentAt: new Date() });
  // à¦‡à¦®à§‡à¦‡à¦² à¦†à¦¸à¦²à§‡ à¦ªà¦¾à¦ à¦¾à¦¨à§‹ à¦¹à¦¬à§‡ à¦¬à§à¦¯à¦¾à¦š à§§à§«-à¦¤à§‡ (Resend), à¦à¦–à¦¨ à¦¶à§à¦§à§ à¦¸à¦¾à¦°à¦¿ à¦œà¦®à¦¾ à¦¥à¦¾à¦•à§‡
  if (wantEmail) rows.push({ ...base, channel: "EMAIL" as const });
  if (rows.length > 0) await prisma.notification.createMany({ data: rows });
  return rows.length;
}

class NotificationService {
  async list(unreadOnly = false, take = 30) {
    const user = await requireUser();
    return prisma.notification.findMany({
      where: {
        recipientId: user.id,
        channel: "IN_APP",
        ...(unreadOnly ? { readAt: null } : {}),
      },
      orderBy: { createdAt: "desc" },
      take,
    });
  }

  async unreadCount() {
    const user = await requireUser();
    return prisma.notification.count({
      where: { recipientId: user.id, channel: "IN_APP", readAt: null },
    });
  }

  async markRead(id: string) {
    const user = await requireUser();
    await prisma.notification.updateMany({
      where: { id, recipientId: user.id, readAt: null },
      data: { readAt: new Date() },
    });
  }

  async markAllRead() {
    const user = await requireUser();
    await prisma.notification.updateMany({
      where: { recipientId: user.id, readAt: null },
      data: { readAt: new Date() },
    });
  }

  async getPreferences() {
    const user = await requireUser();
    return prisma.notificationPreference.findMany({ where: { userId: user.id } });
  }

  async setPreference(type: NotificationType, inApp: boolean, email: boolean) {
    const user = await requireUser();
    return prisma.notificationPreference.upsert({
      where: { userId_type: { userId: user.id, type } },
      update: { inApp, email },
      create: { userId: user.id, type, inApp, email },
    });
  }
}

export const notificationService = new NotificationService();
